```
对 SandboxWorld(game/ 目录)做一次全系统性能异常点扫描——找"可能引起性能异常的点":偶发卡顿、内存泄漏、缓存无淘汰、帧率随时间劣化、交互毛刺。不是稳态主循环优化(刚审计过,余量 5-7×),而是找**异常态**和**慢性劣化**。

扫描范围(每个都带 文件:行号 证据):
1. **缓存无淘汰/内存增长**:
   - ChunkCache(已知 perf-audit 列过"ChunkCache无淘汰"——修了吗?现状多少 chunk 上限、会不会涨);
   - Map/Set 无界增长:tintCache(BiomeBackground drawTiledTinted)、frameCache(projFrameImg)、spriteCache/hellImgs/imgs、netNpcPuppets/netDropSeen/netProjPuppets(联机)、geyserCd、listener 注册泄漏(TileStore.listeners 只加不减?)、checking/skip(LiquidSim 复用?)、lang warn set、warned set;
   - TypedArray 每次分配热点:emitTileParticles Int16Array、entities.all() spread(已知)、dmgNumbers/particles 对象、MessageChannel/postMessage 往返;
   - 贴图/音频:Atlas vimages(解 1 万张 PNG?内存占用)、AudioBuffer(每个 wav 一个)、l10n 包(12 语言全载还是当前语?)。
2. **帧率随时间劣化源**:
   - particles 600 splice(每 splice O(n)——满了以后每次 spawn 都 splice 0,100?看具体);
   - dmgNumbers 同款;
   - hitTiles/wire 冷却表清理;
   - enemies 桶 grows(活跃怪上限?);
   - chunks.dirtyQueue 持续堆积(标记速度>6ms budget 消费速度时怎么退化——死循环丢帧还是无限涨?);
   - lighting perFrame 点光数组。
3. **交互毛刺/偶发卡顿**:
   - 存档 saveGame 主线程结构化克隆 15ms(SaveClient 何时跑——自动存档间隔?跑的时候 UI 冻结吗);
   - 音效首次播放 fetch+decode(已预热关键清单,漏网的会卡一下?)、贴图首次 decode(发光 family?);
   - spawnEnemy/死亡掉落 resolveDrops 的正则表?已退役但确认;
   - largeMap/fullMap 打开时的全图遍历(小地图 redrawAll 50ms 已知,全地图打开一次多贵);
   - Boss 召唤/击败的全屏特效一次性分配。
4. **联机态**(若 net.active):msg 广播频率、AOI、傀儡插值——已有优化批,只找剩余异常点。
5. **移动端/低端**:虚拟摇杆 DOM 更新频率?每帧 style 写?

历史背景(勿重复挖已修):perf-audit-2026-08.md(G9 每帧分配已修/saveGame 已 worker/导入 5 副本已修/contextlost 风暴已修)、multiplayer-capacity-opt-batch.md(P0-P3 已做)、asset-lazy-loading.md(菜单 8300→31 请求)。

产出:按【确认异常(证据+触发条件+影响)】/【疑似(需复现)】/【误报排除(查过没事)】三级输出,每条给严重度(高/中/低)和最便宜的修法方向。不要写代码。

Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.

- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch

1	// chunk 静态缓存：每 chunk 16×16 tile 预渲染到离屏 canvas（墙层+tile 层）
2	import { CHUNK, TILE } from '../core/constants';
3	import { T, TILE_DEFS, WALL_DEFS, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../data/tiles';
4	import { hash2 } from '../core/rng';
5	import { drawVanillaCell, drawTreeCell } from './VanillaTiler';
6	import { swayBakeSkip } from './WindSway';
7	import { TILE_ANIM_RATE, tileAnim, animYOffset, campfireYOffset } from './TileAnim';
8	import { VanillaWallTiler, wallAnimRate } from './VanillaWallTiler';
9	import { shade } from '../assets/Palette';
10	import { paintColor } from '../world/Paint';
11	import type { TileSheetEntry } from '../assets/TileSheetGen';
12	import type { AutoTiler } from './AutoTiler';
13	import type { World } from '../world/World';
14	
15	// 我们的 tile → Maples RuleTile 规则名（有规则的走素材自动贴合，无规则回退程序化）
16	// Grass 走人工标注（'@grass'）：Tiles_2.png 是多 tile 混排图集，自动分类不可靠；
17	// 用 public/annotator.html 标注后写入 sprites/annotations.json。无标注时回退程序化。
18	const TILE_RULES: Record<number, string> = {
19	  // 泥土/石/草走原版 BlendRules；铁矿走原版表——均不再用 Maples 规则
20	  13: '工作台', 14: '熔炉', 15: '铁砧',
21	};
22	
23	export interface ChunkPair {
24	  wall: HTMLCanvasElement;   // 背景墙层（水画在它之上）
25	  tile: HTMLCanvasElement;   // 前景 tile/物体层（画在水之上）
26	}
27	
28	// ---- 油漆乘色着色画布（ChunkCache 静态烘焙消费，world/Paint.applyPaintTint） ----
29	// 原版走 GPU shader（TilePaintSystemV2.cs:69-82）；Canvas 2D 用三段合成等价实现：
30	//   ① 摘出待着色区域 → ② multiply（负相 30 用 difference 反转）填色 →
31	//   ③ destination-in 按原区域 alpha 裁回（multiply 会把透明像素变成实色，必须裁）
32	// 全局单例：每 chunk 烘焙是串行的，16×16 复用零分配
33	const tintCanvas = typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.createElement('canvas') : null;
34	if (tintCanvas) { tintCanvas.width = TILE; tintCanvas.height = TILE; }
35	const tintCtx = tintCanvas ? tintCanvas.getContext('2d') : null;
36	
37	/** 对 canvas 的 (px,py) 16×16 区域按 paint 着色（就地回写） */
38	function tintRegion(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, src: HTMLCanvasElement, px: number, py: number, paint: number): void {
39	  if (!tintCtx || !tintCanvas) return;
40	  tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
41	  tintCtx.clearRect(0, 0, TILE, TILE);
42	  tintCtx.drawImage(src, px, py, TILE, TILE, 0, 0, TILE, TILE);
43	  if (paint === 30) {
44	    // 负相漆：difference × 白 = 反转（MapHelper.MapColor :1849-1851 同式）
45	    tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'difference';
46	    tintCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
47	  } else {
48	    // 其余：multiply × paintColor = 逐通道乘色（白漆 26 乘白 ≡ 原色，自然等价）
49	    tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
50	    const [tr, tg, tb] = paintColor(paint);
51	    tintCtx.fillStyle = `rgb(${tr},${tg},${tb})`;
52	  }
53	  tintCtx.fillRect(0, 0, TILE, TILE);
54	  tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
55	  tintCtx.drawImage(src, px, py, TILE, TILE, 0, 0, TILE, TILE);
56	  tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
57	  // 不 clearRect 直接覆盖回写：着色像素与本区域原内容 alpha 完全一致，
58	  // source-over 叠加即替换色值；clearRect 反而会打穿邻格溢出的跨格精灵
59	  ctx.drawImage(tintCanvas, px, py);
60	}
61	
62	export class ChunkCache {
63	  chunks = new Map<number, ChunkPair>();
64	  dirtyQueue: number[] = [];
65	  sheets: Map<number, TileSheetEntry>;
66	  world: World;
67	  autotiler: AutoTiler | null;
68	  wallTiler: VanillaWallTiler | null;
69	  truncatesWalls: number[] = [];
70	  /** 含动画 tile 的 chunk，按 sheet 分组（换帧时只重建对应 chunk，避免全量重烘焙） */
71	  private animChunksBySheet = new Map<number, Set<number>>();
72	  /** 含动画墙的 chunk，按 wallId 分组（墙无 sheet 概念；DoUpdate_AnimateWalls 的
73	   *  11 类换带墙 + 242/243 星彩玻璃逐格错相——换带时只重建对应 chunk） */
74	  private animChunksByWall = new Map<number, Set<number>>();
75	  /** LRU 上限:每 chunk 2×256² canvas = 512KB;384 chunk ≈ 196MB(缩放 0.5 时
76	   *  可视 ~100 chunk 仍绰绰有余)。此前 Map 只增不减——跑图积累无界(内存泄漏 #1) */
77	  static readonly MAX_CHUNKS = 384;
78	  /** 最近一次 flushDirty 实测耗时 ms（F5 调试报告：烘焙尖峰证据面） */
79	  lastFlushMs = 0;
80	  lastFlushCount = 0;
81	
82	  /** 释放全部 chunk 画布 GPU 背板并清表(退出世界必须调用)。
83	   *  detached canvas 的回收依赖 GC 且明显滞后——连续多次读档累积数百 MB
84	   *  显存,最终 contextlost/contextrestored 风暴卡死(2026-08-10 trace 实证) */
85	  dispose(): void {
86	    for (const pair of this.chunks.values()) {
87	      pair.wall.width = 0; pair.wall.height = 0;
88	      pair.tile.width = 0; pair.tile.height = 0;
89	    }
90	    this.chunks.clear();
91	    this.dirtyQueue.length = 0;
92	    this.animChunksBySheet.clear();
93	    this.animChunksByWall.clear();
94	  }
95	
96	  constructor(world: World, sheets: Map<number, TileSheetEntry>, autotiler: AutoTiler | null = null, wallTiler: VanillaWallTiler | null = null) {
97	    this.world = world;
98	    this.sheets = sheets;
99	    this.autotiler = autotiler;
100	    this.wallTiler = wallTiler;
101	    // 原版 TileID.Sets.TruncatesWalls（54 玻璃 / 328 暗影宝箱 / 459 / 748）→ 内部 tile id
102	    this.truncatesWalls = ['glass', 'v_328_shadow_chest', 'v_459_', 'v_748_']
103	      .map((k) => TILE_BY_KEY[k] ?? -1)
104	      .filter((id) => id >= 0);
105	    world.store.onTileChanged((x, y) => this.markDirtyAround(x, y));
106	  }
107	
108	  static key(cx: number, cy: number): number {
109	    return (cx & 0xffff) | ((cy & 0xffff) << 16);
110	  }
111	
112	  markDirty(cx: number, cy: number) {
113	    const k = ChunkCache.key(cx, cy);
114	    if (!this.chunks.has(k)) return; // 未生成的 chunk 惰性创建
115	    this.chunks.set(k, undefined as unknown as ChunkPair); // 标记删除，重绘时重建
116	    if (!this.dirtyQueue.includes(k)) this.dirtyQueue.push(k);
117	  }
118	
119	  /** 区域标脏（tile 范围）：供树冠等大范围精灵清理使用 */
120	  markDirtyArea(x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number) {
121	    for (let cy = Math.floor(y0 / CHUNK); cy <= Math.floor(y1 / CHUNK); cy++) {
122	      for (let cx = Math.floor(x0 / CHUNK); cx <= Math.floor(x1 / CHUNK); cx++) {
123	        if (cx < 0 || cy < 0) continue;
124	        this.markDirty(cx, cy);
125	      }
126	    }
127	  }
128	
129	  markDirtyAround(x: number, y: number) {
130	    const cx = Math.floor(x / CHUNK), cy = Math.floor(y / CHUNK);
131	    this.markDirty(cx, cy);
132	    // 边缘融合：邻接 chunk 也要标脏
133	    if (x % CHUNK === 0) this.markDirty(cx - 1, cy);
134	    if (x % CHUNK === CHUNK - 1) this.markDirty(cx + 1, cy);
135	    if (y % CHUNK === 0) this.markDirty(cx, cy - 1);
136	    if (y % CHUNK === CHUNK - 1) this.markDirty(cx, cy + 1);
137	  }
138	
139	  /** 全量标脏(atlas 懒加载晚到的新表 → 已烘焙的 chunk 里可能烤了 fallback)。
140	   *  4/帧 的 flushDirty 会逐步重烘焙,dirtyQueue.includes 去重防重复入队 */
141	  invalidateAll(): void {
142	    for (const k of this.chunks.keys()) {
143	      if (this.chunks.get(k) === undefined) continue; // 已是待重建哨兵
144	      this.chunks.set(k, undefined as unknown as ChunkPair);
145	      if (!this.dirtyQueue.includes(k)) this.dirtyQueue.push(k);
146	    }
147	  }
148	
149	  /** 每帧重绘脏 chunk:数量上限 maxN 之外再加时间预算 budgetMs——
150	   *  跑图/全量标脏时烘焙突发不再挤占帧预算(实测 87ms 尖峰来源) */
151	  flushDirty(maxN = 4, budgetMs = 6) {
152	    let n = 0;
153	    const t0 = performance.now();
154	    while (this.dirtyQueue.length && n < maxN) {
155	      const k = this.dirtyQueue.shift()!;
156	      const cx = k & 0xffff, cy = (k >> 16) & 0xffff;
157	      if (this.chunks.get(k) !== undefined) continue; // 已重建
158	      this.get(cx, cy);
159	      n++;
160	      if (performance.now() - t0 > budgetMs) break; // 单 chunk 烘焙超预算也至少完成 1 个
161	    }
162	    this.lastFlushMs = performance.now() - t0;
163	    this.lastFlushCount = n;
164	  }
165	
166	  /** 动画时钟推进（Game 每帧调用）：sheet/wallId 到达换帧行 tick → 只重建对应 chunk。
167	   *  原版语义 = AnimateTiles / DoUpdate_AnimateWalls 每 rate tick 推进一帧；
168	   *  帧内 chunk 复用零开销 */
169	  advanceAnim(): void {
170	    tileAnim.tick++;
171	    if (this.animChunksBySheet.size) {
172	      for (const [sheet, set] of this.animChunksBySheet) {
173	        const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet];
174	        if (!rate || tileAnim.tick % rate !== 0) continue;
175	        for (const k of set) this.markDirty(k & 0xffff, (k >> 16) & 0xffff);
176	      }
177	    }
178	    if (this.animChunksByWall.size) {
179	      for (const [wallId, set] of this.animChunksByWall) {
180	        const rate = wallAnimRate(wallId);
181	        if (!rate || tileAnim.tick % rate !== 0) continue;
182	        for (const k of set) this.markDirty(k & 0xffff, (k >> 16) & 0xffff);
183	      }
184	    }
185	  }
186	
187	  /** 取 chunk 双层画布（惰性生成，LRU 淘汰最久未用） */
188	  get(cx: number, cy: number): ChunkPair {
189	    const k = ChunkCache.key(cx, cy);
190	    let c = this.chunks.get(k);
191	    if (c) {
192	      // LRU:命中即刷新 recency(delete+set 移到 Map 尾部)。渲染循环每帧都 get
193	      // 可视 chunk,故屏上 chunk 永远最新、不会被误淘汰
194	      this.chunks.delete(k);
195	      this.chunks.set(k, c);
196	      return c;
197	    }
198	    c = this.renderChunk(cx, cy);
199	    this.chunks.set(k, c);
200	    while (this.chunks.size > ChunkCache.MAX_CHUNKS) {
201	      const oldest = this.chunks.keys().next().value as number | undefined;
202	      if (oldest === undefined) break;
203	      this.chunks.delete(oldest);
204	    }
205	    return c;
206	  }
207	
208	  /** 树枝判定：TREE 且上下皆非 TREE、恰好一侧为 TREE（横向独连树干）。
209	   *  下方是实心地面的属于树根底座 —— 走规则表渲染底座贴图，不算枝干 */
210	
211	  private neighborMask(x: number, y: number, type: number): number {
212	    const st = this.world.store;
213	    let mask = 0;
214	    const same = (nx: number, ny: number) => st.inBounds(nx, ny) && st.flags[st.idx(nx, ny)] && st.type[st.idx(nx, ny)] === type ? 1 : 0;
215	    mask |= same(x, y - 1);        // N
216	    mask |= same(x + 1, y) << 1;   // E
217	    mask |= same(x, y + 1) << 2;   // S
218	    mask |= same(x - 1, y) << 3;   // W
219	    mask |= same(x + 1, y - 1) << 4; // NE
220	    mask |= same(x + 1, y + 1) << 5; // SE
221	    mask |= same(x - 1, y + 1) << 6; // SW
222	    mask |= same(x - 1, y - 1) << 7; // NW
223	    return mask;
224	  }
225	
226	  private renderChunk(cx: number, cy: number): ChunkPair {
227	    // 双层画布：墙层 / tile 层分离 —— 水渲染在两层之间（墙之上、图块之下）
228	    const wall = document.createElement('canvas');
229	    wall.width = CHUNK * TILE; wall.height = CHUNK * TILE;
230	    const tile = document.createElement('canvas');
231	    tile.width = CHUNK * TILE; tile.height = CHUNK * TILE;
232	    let ctx = wall.getContext('2d')!;
233	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
234	    const wctx = ctx; // 墙层上下文（tile 层重赋 ctx 后油漆 pass 仍需引用）
235	    const st = this.world.store;
236	    const x0 = cx * CHUNK, y0 = cy * CHUNK;
237	
238	    // ---- 第一遍：背景墙全部先画（避免后格的墙盖住跨格物体如宝箱/树冠）----
239	    // 原版墙 framing（VanillaWallTiler）：32×32 帧以格为中心外溢 8px →
240	    // 扫描范围外扩 1 格，跨 chunk 边界的帧由相邻 chunk 补齐（像素一致无副作用）
241	    if (this.wallTiler) {
242	      const EXT = 1;
243	      for (let ly = -EXT; ly < CHUNK + EXT; ly++) {
244	        for (let lx = -EXT; lx < CHUNK + EXT; lx++) {
245	          const x = x0 + lx, y = y0 + ly;
246	          if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
247	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
248	          const wallId = st.wall[i];
249	          if (wallId === 0) continue;
250	          const px = lx * TILE, py = ly * TILE;
251	          if (this.wallTiler.hasTexture(wallId)) {
252	            this.wallTiler.draw(ctx, st, x, y, wallId, this.truncatesWalls, px, py);
253	            // 动画墙（DoUpdate_AnimateWalls 换带 + 星彩玻璃逐格错相）：登记进换带
254	            // 重烘焙行列——墙无 sheet 概念，按 wallId 另建 map（tiles 侧同款机制）
255	            if (wallAnimRate(wallId) !== 0) {
256	              let wset = this.animChunksByWall.get(wallId);
257	              if (!wset) { wset = new Set(); this.animChunksByWall.set(wallId, wset); }
258	              wset.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));
259	            }
260	          } else {
261	            const wd = WALL_DEFS[wallId];
262	            if (wd) {
263	              ctx.fillStyle = wd.mapColor;
264	              ctx.fillRect(px, py, TILE, TILE);
265	              ctx.fillStyle = shade(wd.mapColor, 0.8);
266	              ctx.fillRect(px, py + TILE - 1, TILE, 1);
267	              ctx.fillRect(px + TILE - 1, py, 1, TILE);
268	            }
269	          }
270	        }
271	      }
272	    }
273	
274	    // ---- 第二遍：前景 tile / 物体（绘制到 tile 层画布；水渲染在墙层与 tile 层之间）----
275	    ctx = tile.getContext('2d')!;
276	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
277	    for (let ly = 0; ly < CHUNK; ly++) {
278	      for (let lx = 0; lx < CHUNK; lx++) {
279	        const x = x0 + lx, y = y0 + ly;
280	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
281	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
282	        const px = lx * TILE, py = ly * TILE;
283	        const type = st.type[i];
284	        // 原版语义:非活性格不渲染(TileRunner 会给空气格写幽灵 type)
285	        if (type === 0 || !st.flags[i]) continue;
286	        // 已致动(inActive):幽灵态淡显(原版 DrawInactiveSorter,Main.cs:2828 附近)
287	        // 每格开头统一设定 alpha(各 continue 路径无需逐个恢复,下一格自愈)
288	        const actuated = (st.wire[i] & 32) !== 0;
289	        ctx.globalAlpha = actuated ? 0.3 : 1;
290	        const def = TILE_DEFS[type];
291	        if (!def) { ctx.fillStyle = '#808080'; ctx.fillRect(px, py, TILE, TILE); continue; }
292	        // 风摆动图块（草/藤/吊挂植物/树冠标记帧）：摘出静态烘焙，
293	        // 由 Renderer 的 WindSway overlay 逐帧动态绘制（原版 AddSpecialPoint 特殊路径）
294	        if (def.vanilla && swayBakeSkip(type, st.frameX[i])) continue;
295	        // 原版素材图块（TileDef.vanilla）：TEdit framing 查找表（auto）或显式 18px 帧（style）
296	        if (def.vanilla && this.autotiler) {
297	          // 动画陈设（原版 AnimateTiles/GetTileDrawData addFrY）：frameY += 帧索引*pitch
298	          // （pitch 默认 38，3 格高特例组 54，篝火族特例 36+熄灭行静止——见 TileAnim）
299	          let fy = st.frameY[i];
300	          if (def.vanilla.sheet === 215) {
301	            fy += campfireYOffset(st.frameY[i]);
302	            let set215 = this.animChunksBySheet.get(215);
303	            if (!set215) { set215 = new Set(); this.animChunksBySheet.set(215, set215); }
304	            set215.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));
305	          } else if (def.vanilla.sheet === 314) {
306	            // 矿车轨道加速带动画（Main.cs:18734-18741 每 10 tick 推进、5 帧回卷）：
307	            // 只注册重烘焙——frameY 是后轨连接 ID，勿走 addFrY 帧偏移；
308	            // 帧行偏移由 drawMinecartTrackCell → sourceRectOf(frameID, anim) 处理
309	            let set314 = this.animChunksBySheet.get(314);
310	            if (!set314) { set314 = new Set(); this.animChunksBySheet.set(314, set314); }
311	            set314.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));
312	          } else if (TILE_ANIM_RATE[def.vanilla.sheet]) {
313	            const rows = this.autotiler.atlas.vmeta(def.vanilla.sheet)?.rows ?? 0;
314	            fy += animYOffset(def.vanilla.sheet, rows * 18);
315	            let set = this.animChunksBySheet.get(def.vanilla.sheet);
316	            if (!set) { set = new Set(); this.animChunksBySheet.set(def.vanilla.sheet, set); }
317	            set.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));
318	          }
319	          drawVanillaCell(
320	            ctx, this.autotiler.atlas, def.vanilla.sheet, def.vanilla.frame,
321	            def.vanilla.fw ?? 1, def.vanilla.fh ?? 1,
322	            st, x, y, type,
323	            (t) => t === type, // 同 id 融合判定（后续可扩 mergeWith）
324	            px, py, st.frameX[i], fy,
325	            { treeX: this.world.treeX, treeStyle: this.world.treeStyle, treeTops: this.world.treeTops,
326	              worldSurface: this.world.groundLevel, worldW: this.world.w },
327	          );
328	          continue;
329	        }
330	        // 树苗：Tree_Bodys 树干段作小苗（底部对齐）
331	        if (type === T.SAPLING && this.autotiler) {
332	          const r = this.autotiler.saplingSprite(x, y);
333	          if (r) {
334	            ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, px + (TILE - r.sw) / 2, py + TILE - r.sh, r.sw, r.sh);
335	            continue;
336	          }
337	        }
338	        // 杂草：Maples Tiles_3 杂草贴图（16×20，底部对齐，hash 选变体）
339	        if (type === T.TALLGRASS && this.autotiler) {
340	          const r = this.autotiler.weedSprite(x, y);
341	          if (r) {
342	            ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, px - (r.sw - TILE) / 2, py + TILE - r.sh, r.sw, r.sh);
343	            continue;
344	          }
345	        }
346	        // 有 RuleTile 规则的 tile 用 Maples 素材自动贴合
347	        const ruleName = this.autotiler ? TILE_RULES[type] : undefined;
348	        if (ruleName && this.autotiler) {
349	          // 草皮覆盖件：保持原生透明（缺口露出背后的墙/天空），不做任何垫底/填充
350	          if (ruleName === '@grass') {
351	            const r = this.autotiler.tile(ruleName, st, x, y, type);
352	            if (r) ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, px, py, TILE, TILE);
353	            continue;
354	          }
355	          const r = this.autotiler.tile(ruleName, st, x, y, type);
356	          if (r) {
357	            // 大图（树冠 80×80，宽>2格）跳过 —— 第三遍统一绘制（跨 chunk 补全 + 树叶盖树干）
358	            if (r.sw > TILE * 2) continue;
359	            // 按精灵原始尺寸绘制。树干等"宽≤2格、高>1格"的竖向件顶部对齐：
360	            // 溢出向下伸，由更下方的格（后画）覆盖 —— 下层不压上层。
361	            let dy = py + (TILE - r.sh) / 2;
362	            if (r.sh > TILE && r.sw <= TILE * 2) dy = py;
363	            const dx = px + (TILE - r.sw) / 2;
364	            ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, dx, dy, r.sw, r.sh);
365	            continue;
366	          }
367	        }
368	        const entry = this.sheets.get(type);
369	        if (!entry) {
370	          ctx.fillStyle = '#FF00FF';
371	          ctx.fillRect(px, py, TILE, TILE);
372	          continue;
373	        }
374	        if (entry.kind === 'blend') {
375	          const mask = this.neighborMask(x, y, type);
376	          const col = mask & 15, row = mask >> 4;
377	          ctx.drawImage(entry.canvas, col * TILE, row * TILE, TILE, TILE, px, py, TILE, TILE);
378	        } else if (entry.kind === 'object') {
379	          // 仅锚点绘制整体
380	          if (st.frameX[i] === 0 && st.frameY[i] === 0) {
381	            const w = (entry.w ?? 1) * TILE, h = (entry.h ?? 1) * TILE;
382	            ctx.drawImage(entry.canvas, px, py, w, h);
383	          }
384	        } else {
385	          // single：帧偏移直接取
386	          ctx.drawImage(entry.canvas, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i], TILE, TILE, px, py, TILE, TILE);
387	        }
388	      }
389	    }
390	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1; // 致动幽灵 alpha 复位(防影响后续 pass)
391	
392	    // ---- 油漆乘色 pass：墙层 + tile 层分别着色 ----
393	    // 原版 tile/wall 各自经 tileShader 按格取 paint（TilePaintSystemV2）；
394	    // Canvas 等价实现见 tintRegion（乘色模型见 world/Paint.applyPaintTint 注释）。
395	    // ★ 等价边界：原版按"绘制调用"着色（多格物件整张贴图随锚格上色）；
396	    //   本实现按 16×16 格区域着色——涂多格家具/树只有被涂格区域显色（登记）
397	    for (let ly = 0; ly < CHUNK; ly++) {
398	      for (let lx = 0; lx < CHUNK; lx++) {
399	        const x = x0 + lx, y = y0 + ly;
400	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
401	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
402	        const pw = st.paintWall[i];
403	        if (pw > 0 && st.wall[i] !== 0) tintRegion(wctx, wall, lx * TILE, ly * TILE, pw);
404	        const pt = st.paint[i];
405	        if (pt > 0 && st.flags[i] && st.type[i] !== 0) tintRegion(ctx, tile, lx * TILE, ly * TILE, pt);
406	      }
407	    }
408	
409	    // ---- 第三遍：半砖（halfBrick）——主绘制后清掉上半 8px ----
410	    // VanillaTiler blend/auto/style 三路径已按原版源矩形裁剪（源 y+8 高-8）；
411	    // 此处 clearRect 仅作兜底（uv 查找失败走 vframe(1,1) 全帧回退等路径仍画满 16×16）
412	    for (let ly = 0; ly < CHUNK; ly++) {
413	      for (let lx = 0; lx < CHUNK; lx++) {
414	        const i = st.idx(x0 + lx, y0 + ly);
415	        if (st.half[i]) ctx.clearRect(lx * TILE, ly * TILE, TILE, 8);
416	      }
417	    }
418	
419	    // ---- 第四遍：树静态部分（跨 chunk 外扩绘制） ----
420	    // 风摆动系统接管后：树冠/树枝标记帧不再烘焙（Renderer WindSway overlay 逐帧摆动），
421	    // 本遍只保留棕榈干身（倾斜跨列必须外扩遍）与蘑菇树顶（72 原版不摆动）。
422	    if (this.autotiler) {
423	      const treeIds = ['v_72_mushroom_tree', 'v_323_palm_trees']
424	        .map((k) => TILE_BY_KEY[k]).filter((id) => id !== undefined);
425	      for (const v5 of treeIds) {
426	        const EXT = 6;
427	        for (let ty = y0 - EXT; ty < y0 + CHUNK + EXT; ty++) {
428	          for (let tx = x0 - EXT; tx < x0 + CHUNK + EXT; tx++) {
429	            if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
430	            const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
431	            if (st.type[i] !== v5) continue;
432	            const fx = st.frameX[i], fy = st.frameY[i];
433	            const def = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]];
434	            const sheet = def?.vanilla?.sheet ?? 5;
435	            const trees = { treeX: this.world.treeX, treeStyle: this.world.treeStyle, treeTops: this.world.treeTops,
436	              worldSurface: this.world.groundLevel, worldW: this.world.w };
437	            if (sheet === 323) {
438	              const isCrown = fx >= 88 && fx <= 132;
439	              if (isCrown) continue; // 棕榈冠 → WindSway overlay
440	              // 棕榈干身（倾斜跨列，必须外扩遍）
441	              drawTreeCell(ctx, this.autotiler.atlas, st, tx, ty, fx, fy,
442	                tx * TILE - x0 * TILE, ty * TILE - y0 * TILE, sheet, false, trees);
443	            } else {
444	              // 蘑菇树：顶格标记帧原版不摆动，保留烘焙（DrawTrees switch 无 case 72）
445	              drawTreeCell(ctx, this.autotiler.atlas, st, tx, ty, fx, fy,
446	                tx * TILE - x0 * TILE, ty * TILE - y0 * TILE, sheet, true, trees);
447	            }
448	          }
449	        }
450	      }
451	    }
452	
453	    return { wall, tile };
454	  }
455	}
456	

```

90	  }

92	  idx(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.w + x; }
93	
94	  inBounds(x: number, y: number): boolean {
95	    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < this.w && y < this.h;
96	  }
97	
98	  get(x: number, y: number): number { return this.inBounds(x, y) ? this.type[this.idx(x, y)] : 0; }
99	
100	  isActive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
101	    // 原版 nactive() = active && !inActive（Tile.cs:383-394）：已致动格视为不活跃
102	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && this.type[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0
103	      && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) === 0;
104	  }
105	
106	  /** 原版 inActive()：致动器翻转后的"缩入地下"态（有 type 但不碰撞不阻挡） */
107	  isActuated(x: number, y: number): boolean {
108	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) !== 0;
109	  }
110	
111	  isSolid(x: number, y: number): boolean {
112	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false; // 世界外视为空（边缘自由）
113	    if (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) return false; // 已致动不碰撞
114	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
115	    return !!d && d.solid;
116	  }
117	
118	  isPlatform(x: number, y: number): boolean {
119	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
120	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
121	    return !!d && d.platform;
122	  }
123	
124	  onTileChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
125	    this.listeners.push(fn);
126	  }
127	
128	  /** 液体变化独立通道：chunk 缓存不订阅（水不入烘焙画布，无需重建），
129	   *  只有光照订阅（水的光衰减）——避免水流时每帧冲爆 chunk 重建队列造成卡顿 */
130	  private liquidListeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];
131	  onLiquidChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
132	    this.liquidListeners.push(fn);
133	  }
134	
135	  /** 唯一的写入入口（生成/导入期可绕过用 setTileSilent） */
136	  setTile(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
137	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
138	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
139	    const wasActive = this.flags[i] !== 0;
140	    this.type[i] = type;
141	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
142	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
143	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
144	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除清半砖/坡面
145	    // 油漆随方块清除/铺块重置：
146	    //   KillTile（WorldGen.cs:63933-63939）active(false)+halfBrick(false)+ClearBlockPaintAndCoating；
147	    //   PlaceTile（WorldGen.cs:59499-59501）!active() 时 Clear(Tile|TilePaint|Slope)。
148	    //   即油漆只在 active→active 的替换（草化/转化）中存活
149	    if (type === 0 || !wasActive) this.paint[i] = 0;
150	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetTile, x, y, v: type, fx: frameX, fy: frameY });
151	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
152	  }
153	
154	  /** 运行期改半砖/坡面位（锤子交互用；生成期直接写数组即可） */
155	  setHalfBrick(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
156	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
157	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
158	    this.half[i] = v ? 1 : 0;
159	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetHalfBrick, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
160	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
161	  }
162	
163	  setSlope(x: number, y: number, s: number) {
164	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
165	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
166	    this.slope[i] = s;
167	    this.half[i] = 0; // 原版 SlopeTile 无条件 halfBrick(false)（WorldGen.cs:49174），slope=0 复位时同样
168	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetSlope, x, y, v: s, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
169	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
170	  }
171	
172	  setTileSilent(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
173	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
174	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
175	    const wasActive = this.flags[i] !== 0;
176	    this.type[i] = type;
177	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
178	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
179	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
180	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除同步清位（防生成期脏残留）
181	    if (type === 0 || !wasActive) this.paint[i] = 0;          // KillTile/PlaceTile 油漆清位（同 setTile）
182	  }
183	
184	  setWall(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
185	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
186	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
187	    const hadWall = this.wall[i] !== 0;
188	    if (this.wall[i] !== wall) {
189	      this.wall[i] = wall;
190	      // 墙漆清位：KillWall（WorldGen.cs:60425-60426）wall=0 后 ClearWallPaintAndCoating；
191	      // PlaceWall（:75199-75201）本身不清 wallColor——但 KillWall 已保证 wall==0 ⇒ 无漆，
192	      // 故 0→非0 铺墙时同样归零（保持原版不可达态不变量，见 world/Paint.ts）
193	      if (wall === 0 || !hadWall) this.paintWall[i] = 0;
194	      if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWall, x, y, v: wall, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
195	      this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
196	    }
197	  }
198	
199	  setWallSilent(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
200	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
201	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
202	    const hadWall = this.wall[i] !== 0;
203	    this.wall[i] = wall;
204	    if (wall === 0 || !hadWall) this.paintWall[i] = 0; // 同 setWall 的墙漆清位
205	  }
206	
207	  /** 油漆写入（WorldGen.paintTile/paintWall 等价原语）。发 listeners：油漆改变 tile/wall
208	   *  视觉色 → chunk 画布与小地图都须重建（导线不进烘焙画布故 setWire 不发，此处不同）。
209	   *  值域 0-31（PaintID），>31 由调用方保证不出现（Item.paint 为 byte） */
210	  setPaint(x: number, y: number, v: number) {
211	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
212	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
213	    if (this.paint[i] === v) return;
214	    this.paint[i] = v & 31;
215	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
216	  }
217	
218	  setPaintSilent(x: number, y: number, v: number) {
219	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
220	    this.paint[this.idx(x, y)] = v & 31;
221	  }
222	
223	  /** 墙漆写入（WorldGen.paintWall） */
224	  setPaintWall(x: number, y: number, v: number) {
225	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
226	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
227	    if (this.paintWall[i] === v) return;
228	    this.paintWall[i] = v & 31;
229	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
230	  }
231	
232	  setPaintWallSilent(x: number, y: number, v: number) {
233	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
234	    this.paintWall[this.idx(x, y)] = v & 31;
235	  }
236	
237	  /** 导线/致动位写入（Wiring 系统）。不发 listeners：导线不进 chunk 烘焙画布，
238	   *  走 Renderer 动态覆盖层；致动位变化才需要标脏 chunk（调用方负责） */
239	  setWire(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
240	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
241	    this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] = bits & 63;
242	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWire, x, y, v: bits & 63, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
243	  }
244	
245	  setWireSilent(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
246	    this.setWire(x, y, bits);
247	  }
248	
249	  /** 致动器翻转 inActive 态（Wiring.DeActive/ReActive 用）：影响碰撞与渲染，须发 listeners */
250	  setActuated(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
251	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
252	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
253	    const b = v ? (this.wire[i] | TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) : (this.wire[i] & ~TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED);
254	    if (this.wire[i] === b) return;
255	    this.wire[i] = b;
256	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetActuated, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
257	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
258	  }
259	
260	  setLiquid(x: number, y: number, amount: number, type: number) {
261	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
262	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
263	    const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, amount | 0));
264	    if (this.liquid[i] === a && this.liquidType[i] === type) return;
265	    this.liquid[i] = a;
266	    this.liquidType[i] = type;
267	    this.liquidListeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
268	  }
269	}

Let's check how often markDirty is called. On tile changes (every setTile). During liquid? No. During explosions? markDirtyArea. Each markDirty → `dirtyQueue.includes` linear scan. With a dirtyQueue of size ~400 (after invalidateAll), each tile change costs 400 comparisons. Moderate.

   Also, LRU eviction: when chunk evicted by LRU while still referenced in dirtyQueue? Not an issue.

   Important: eviction loop `while (this.chunks.size > MAX_CHUNKS)` deletes oldest WITHOUT releasing canvas (pair.wall.width=0). In dispose() they explicitly set width=0 to free GPU backing. But LRU eviction in get() just `this.chunks.delete(oldest)` — canvas not released explicitly. That relies on GC; the comment in dispose says detached canvas reclamation is laggy and caused contextlost storms. So LRU eviction doesn't free canvases → running around a lot could accumulate hundreds of MB of un-released canvases → contextlost risk. That's a genuine issue: **LRU eviction without explicit canvas release**. Severity: 中-高.

   Also note: `renderChunk` creates 2 canvases of 256×256 each (CHUNK*TILE = 16*16 = 256). Comment says "每 chunk 2×256² canvas = 512KB" — 256*256*4 = 262144 bytes = 256KB each, ×2 = 512KB. OK.

   Also animChunksBySheet/animChunksByWall sets: entries added during renderChunk, never removed when chunk evicted or disposed? dispose clears them. But when chunks are evicted by LRU, the anim sets keep stale chunk keys → advanceAnim will markDirty on evicted chunks → markDirty does `if (!this.chunks.has(k)) return;` so it's fine, just set growth. The sets grow with chunk keys; bounded by number of chunks with anim tiles, but never cleaned on eviction → slow growth of sets (bounded by chunks visited over time... actually chunk keys are bounded by world size, so it's bounded by total chunks in world ~ (4200/16)*(1200/16) = 262*75 = 19,650 for a large world. Fine-ish, but dirty anim marks will grow the dirtyQueue over time? markDirty only enqueues if chunks.has(k). Since evicted chunks aren't in map, no enqueue. OK.

   Actually wait — there's a subtle issue: advanceAnim marks dirty every animation tick for each anim chunk in the *visible set only*, since only rendered chunks get registered. Fine.

   Another: markDirty uses `this.dirtyQueue.includes(k)` — O(n) each call. During a liquid flood or a big explosion, thousands of setTile → markDirtyAround calls. dirtyQueue up to a few hundred → includes scan cost. Also flushDirty `shift()` is O(n) each — with 400 dirty entries, that's 400*400/2 = 80k ops worst case, fine.

   Bigger: invalidateAll → dirtyQueue with ~384 entries, flushed at 4/frame with 6ms budget → 96 frames ≈ 1.6s. OK.

   But the question asks: "chunks.dirtyQueue 持续堆积(标记速度>6ms budget 消费速度时怎么退化——死循环丢帧还是无限涨?)" — flushDirty has budgetMs=6 break, so worst case one frame spends >6ms if first chunk is slow (at least 1 chunk). Not a death spiral. But dirtyQueue is unbounded: if marking is faster than 4/frame consumption, it grows unboundedly (e.g., large liquid operations, world corruption spread with many setTile per frame). Also, an important bug: markDirty sets `this.chunks.set(k, undefined as unknown as ChunkPair)` — this *resets* the LRU position? No — `set` on existing key keeps original insertion order in JS Maps! Actually Map.set on an existing key does NOT change iteration order. So the dirty sentinel doesn't affect LRU. But note: `get()` treats `c` falsy → renderChunk. And in flushDirty, `if (this.chunks.get(k) !== undefined) continue;`.

   Hmm wait, there IS an LRU bug: `get()` — when a chunk is a dirty sentinel (undefined), it re-renders and `this.chunks.set(k, c)` — this does NOT refresh recency for the sentinel path! In the hit path they do delete+set to refresh recency, but in the miss path (line 198-199) they just `set` without delete. For an existing sentinel key, insertion order stays at the original position. So a chunk that gets re-rendered through the dirty path doesn't move to the MRU end. Minor.

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T05:53:09.109Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
dmgNumbers uses write-index compaction (good). particles uses splice(0, len-600) — only when over 600, so splice is rare-ish; but with sustained heavy spawning (e.g., rain, meteor, boss), every push after saturation triggers a splice of some amount. Actually splice(0, length-600): if spawn rate is 10/frame, each frame removes 10 → O(n) move of 600 elements each frame = 600*10 = manageable but it's a memmove of ~600 refs = fine. Not a big deal. But splice(0, ...) shifts the array → O(n) each call; called every push-site when saturated. Modest.

Let me look at the particle update loop in Game.ts (around 11958-12200), the particles update compaction, emitTileParticles Int16Array, and the particle spawn functions.

Also check TileParticles.ts.
```

</details>

12117	      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
12118	      const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
12119	      if (st.type[i] !== TORCH_ID) continue;
12120	      if (torchStillAnchored(st, tx, ty, st.frameX[i])) continue;
12121	      st.setTile(tx, ty, 0);
12122	      this.spawnDrop(tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE, VI_KEY.TORCH, 1);
12123	      this.sfx.play('dig');
12124	    }
12125	  }
12126	
12127	  spawnParticles(
12128	    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed = 2,
12129	    opts: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number; size?: number } = {},
12130	  ) {
12131	    const base = opts.life ?? 40;
12132	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
12133	      const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
12134	      const life = Math.round(base * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.4));
12135	      this.particles.push({
12136	        x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * speed * Math.random(), vy: Math.sin(a) * speed * Math.random() - 1,
12137	        life, maxLife: life, color, size: opts.size ?? 2 + Math.random() * 2,
12138	        damp: opts.damp, grav: opts.grav,
12139	      });
12140	    }
12141	    if (this.particles.length > 600) this.particles.splice(0, this.particles.length - 600);
12142	  }
12143	
12144	  /** 定向粒子爆发（GameHooks.spawnBurst）：烟花爆花/喷泉/马桶水花的原版 dust
12145	   *  带初速方向与轴向压缩（Projectile.cs Kill/AI 各循环的速度档与 x/y 乘数）。
12146	   *  角度 = dir ± spread；速度系数 0.85+rand*0.2 对齐原版喷泉的
12147	   *  `spinningpoint *= 0.85 + NextDouble()*0.2`（Projectile.cs:29388-29390 等） */
12148	  spawnBurst(
12149	    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed: number,
12150	    dir: number, spread: number,
12151	    opts: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number; xMul?: number; yMul?: number } = {},
12152	  ) {
12153	    const base = opts.life ?? 40;
12154	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
12155	      const a = dir + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * spread;
12156	      const sp = speed * (0.85 + Math.random() * 0.2);
12157	      const life = Math.round(base * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.4));
12158	      this.particles.push({
12159	        x, y,
12160	        vx: Math.cos(a) * sp * (opts.xMul ?? 1),
12161	        vy: Math.sin(a) * sp * (opts.yMul ?? 1),
12162	        life, maxLife: life, color, size: 2 + Math.random() * 2,
12163	        damp: opts.damp, grav: opts.grav,
12164	      });
12165	    }
12166	    if (this.particles.length > 600) this.particles.splice(0, this.particles.length - 600);
12167	  }
12168	
12169	  setZoom(z: number) {
12170	    if (!this.camera) return;
12171	    this.camera.setZoomTarget(z);
12172	  }
12173	
12174	  // ================= 合成 =================
12175	
12176	  itemId(key: string): number {
12177	    return ITEM_BY_KEY[key];
12178	  }
12179	
12180	  stationsNearby(): Set<string> {
12181	    const st = this.world.store;
12182	    const px = Math.floor(this.player.cx / TILE), py = Math.floor(this.player.cy / TILE);
12183	    const found = new Set<string>(['hand']);
12184	    for (let dx = -4; dx <= 4; dx++) {
12185	      for (let dy = -3; dy <= 3; dy++) {
12186	        const t = st.get(px + dx, py + dy);
12187	        if (t === T.WORKBENCH) found.add('workbench');
12188	        if (t === T.FURNACE) found.add('furnace');
12189	        if (t === T.ANVIL) found.add('anvil');
12190	      }
12191	    }
12192	    return found;
12193	  }
12194	
12195	  // (canCraft/craft 自制配方链已随 RECIPES 表退役移除——合成统一走 vanillaCraft)
12196	
12197	  // ================= 原版配方（1456 全量 2688 条） =================
12198	
12199	  /** 附近站点集合（内部 tile id）+ 液体/群系 → VanillaCraftCtx（每帧缓存由 UI 调用侧刷新） */

```

278	export const TILE_PARTICLE_RULES: Map<number, TileParticleRule[]> = (() => {
279	  const m = new Map<number, TileParticleRule[]>();

281	    const arr = m.get(rule.sheet);
282	    if (arr) arr.push(rule);
283	    else m.set(rule.sheet, [rule]);
284	  }
285	  return m;
286	})();

288	/** 该 tile 帧是否命中规则（帧门判定，供测试） */
289	export function ruleMatches(rule: TileParticleRule, fx: number, fy: number): boolean {
290	  if (rule.fx0 !== undefined && fx !== rule.fx0) return false;
291	  if (rule.fy0 !== undefined && fy !== rule.fy0) return false;
292	  if (rule.pred && !rule.pred(fx, fy)) return false;
293	  return true;
294	}
295	
296	/** 健全性：规则里引用的 sheet 必须已注册 */
297	export function auditRules(): { missing: number[]; total: number } {
298	  const missing: number[] = [];
299	  for (const sheet of TILE_PARTICLE_RULES.keys()) {
300	    const def = TILE_DEFS.find((d) => d.vanilla?.sheet === sheet);
301	    if (!def && TILE_BY_KEY[`v_${sheet}`] === undefined && sheet !== 17 && sheet !== 12
302	      && sheet !== 63 && sheet !== 64 && sheet !== 65 && sheet !== 66 && sheet !== 67 && sheet !== 68
303	      && sheet !== 45 && sheet !== 46 && sheet !== 47 && sheet !== 6 && sheet !== 7 && sheet !== 8 && sheet !== 9) {
304	      missing.push(sheet);
305	    }
306	  }
307	  return { missing, total: TILE_PARTICLE_RULES.size };
308	}

3801	      if (d.alpha >= 1) { d.alpha = 1; d.alphaDir = -1; }
3802	      if (d.dot) d.vy += 0.15;
3803	      else { d.vy *= 0.92; if (d.crit) d.vy *= 0.92; }
3804	      d.vx = (d.vx ?? 0) * 0.93;
3805	      d.x += d.vx;
3806	      d.y += d.vy;
3807	      // 原版先 lifeTime-- 再判 ≤0（:162-163）:寿命 1→0 那一 tick 即进收缩期
3808	      d.life--;
3809	      if (d.life > 0) {
3810	        if (d.crit) d.rotation = (d.rotation ?? 0) + (d.vx < 0 ? 0.001 : -0.001);
3811	        if (d.dot) d.scale = Math.min(0.8, (d.scale ?? 0) + 0.5);
3812	        else d.scale = Math.min(1, (d.scale ?? 0) + 0.1);
3813	        this.dmgNumbers[wn++] = d;
3814	      } else {
3815	        d.scale = (d.scale ?? 1) - 0.1;
3816	        if (d.crit) { d.alphaDir = -1; d.scale += 0.07; }
3817	        if ((d.scale ?? 1) >= 0.1) this.dmgNumbers[wn++] = d; // 收缩期保留,scale<0.1 消亡
3818	      }
3819	    }
3820	    this.dmgNumbers.length = wn;
3821	
3822	    inp.endFrame();
3823	
3824	    // 挥剑命中判定（实体更新后）
3825	    this.postUpdate();
3826	  }
3827	
3828	  // ================= 物品使用：挖掘 / 放置 / 攻击 / 使用 =================
3829	

```

During early exploration as atlas lazy-loads, this could cause repeated churn: every newly loaded Tiles_N invalidates all chunks → 384 chunks re-baked at 4/frame = 96 frames each time. If files arrive frequently, the queue never drains → sustained bake load → frame time degradation for minutes. This is a REAL chronic degradation source. And the dirtyQueue `includes` dedupe is O(n²).

151	/** 整图硬 alpha：alpha ≥128 → 255，<128 → 0（并清零 RGB），消除提取 PNG 的半透明镶边 */
152	function hardAlpha(img: HTMLImageElement): HTMLCanvasElement {
153	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
154	  c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
155	  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
156	  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
157	  const d = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
158	  const px = d.data;
159	  for (let i = 0; i < px.length; i += 4) {
160	    if (px[i + 3] >= 128) px[i + 3] = 255;
161	    else {
162	      px[i] = 0; px[i + 1] = 0; px[i + 2] = 0; px[i + 3] = 0;
163	    }
164	  }
165	  ctx.putImageData(d, 0, 0);
166	  return c;
167	}
168	
169	export class SpriteAtlas {
170	  data = atlasJson as unknown as AtlasData;
171	  resources = resourcesJson as unknown as ResourcesData;
172	  vanilla = vanillaJson as unknown as VanillaData;
173	  images = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement>();
174	  vimages = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement>(); // 原版 PNG（干净像素，不做 hardAlpha）
175	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/，干净像素不 hardAlpha——UI 有抗锯齿边缘） */
176	  uiimages = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement>();
177	  private uiFiles = (vanillaUiJson as { files: Record<string, string> }).files;
178	  /** 人工标注（annotator.html 导出）：sheet → spriteName → 方位标签 */
179	  annotations: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = {};
180	
181	  async load(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
182	    const files = Object.keys(this.data.files);
183	    // 原版 vanilla 素材与 vanilla-ui 贴图全部不在启动预载(8550 请求/主菜单 2GB 根因):
184	    // 图块/墙/NPC 表 → Game.newWorld/loadWorld 里 preloadVanillaWorld() 预载
185	    // (onWorldReady 之前完成,首帧 chunk 烘焙无回退);物品图标 → vicon 按需
186	    // 懒加载 + 进世界后 prefetchIcons() 后台补齐;UI 贴图 → vui() 按需
187	    // 懒加载(全部 11 处消费方每帧重查,首帧 null 自兜底)
188	    const vfiles: string[] = [];
189	    const uifiles: string[] = [];
190	    let done = 0;
191	    const total = files.length + vfiles.length + uifiles.length;
192	    await Promise.all([
193	      ...files.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
194	        const img = new Image();
195	        img.onload = () => {
196	          // 根源处理：整图硬 alpha —— 抗锯齿半透明像素（提取 PNG 的灰/黑镶边来源）
197	          // 二值化为 0/255，所有消费方（tile/墙/图标/角色）统一获得干净像素
198	          this.images.set(f, hardAlpha(img));
199	          done++;
200	          onProgress?.(done / total);
201	          resolve();
202	        };
203	        img.onerror = () => resolve();
204	        img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
205	      })),
206	    ]);
207	    // 人工标注（可选，缺失时回退）
208	    try {
209	      const r = await fetch('sprites/annotations.json');
210	      if (r.ok) this.annotations = await r.json();
211	    } catch { /* 无标注 */ }
212	  }
213	
214	  // ---- 原版素材 API（无 Unity y 翻转，按 TEdit 网格寻址） ----
215	
216	  /** 原版图块元数据 */
217	  vmeta(sheetId: number): VanillaTileMeta | null {
218	    return this.vanilla.tiles[String(sheetId)] ?? null;
219	  }
220	
221	  /** 原版图块表取帧（col,row 从 0 起）。越界/缺失返回 null */
222	  vframe(sheetId: number, col: number, row: number): DrawRect | null {
223	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
224	    if (!m) return null;
225	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);  // 懒加载兜底(正常路径 preloadVanillaWorld 已就绪)
226	    if (!img) return null;
227	    if (col < 0 || row < 0 || col >= m.cols || row >= m.rows) return null;
228	    return { img, sx: col * m.stride[0], sy: row * m.stride[1], sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
229	  }
230	
231	  /** 原版表内任意像素偏移取帧（style/显式帧：18px 步长的 frameX/frameY 直用） */
232	  vframeAt(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number): DrawRect | null {
233	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
234	    if (!m) return null;
235	    // 与 vframe/vrect 同语义懒加载（ensureVImage）：place_v_* 物品图标走本方法,
236	    // 此前直接 vimages.get——表未载时不发加载请求,宝箱内家具类物品图标永久回退
237	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
238	    if (!img) return null;
239	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + m.grid[0] > img.width || fy + m.grid[1] > img.height) return null;
240	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
241	  }
242	
243	  /** 原版表内任意矩形（多格物体整体取图，如墓碑 2×2 = 34×34px） */
244	  vrect(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number, w: number, h: number): DrawRect | null {
245	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
246	    if (!m) return null;
247	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
248	    if (!img) return null;
249	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + w > img.width || fy + h > img.height) return null;
250	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: w, sh: h };
251	  }
252	
253	  /** 原版 NPC 贴图表取帧（纵向帧条，frameIdx 0-based）。
254	   *  未登记的 id 懒加载 vanilla/NPC_{id}.png（帧数来自 vanilla-npcs.json），首帧返回 null 下一帧生效 */
255	  private lazyNpcMeta = new Map<string, VanillaNpcMeta>();
256	  /** ⚠仅适用【纵向帧条】NPC 表。横向变体横条 NPC（如 594 风气球 = 8 列×32px 变体,
257	   *  Main.cs:23383 Frame(8,1,ai[2])）走此路径会把整条横排画出来——此类 NPC 必须
258	   *  在 Renderer.drawEnemy 加专属分支按列切片（见 drawWindyBalloon）。 */
259	  vnpc(npcId: number, frameIdx: number): DrawRect | null {
260	    let m: VanillaNpcMeta | undefined = this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)];
261	    if (!m) {
262	      const key = String(npcId);
263	      m = this.lazyNpcMeta.get(key);
264	      if (!m) {
265	        const sheet = `vanilla/NPC_${npcId}.png`;
266	        const img = this.vimages.get(sheet);
267	        if (!img) {
268	          const im = new Image();
269	          im.onload = () => this.vimages.set(sheet, im);
270	          im.src = `sprites/${sheet}`;
271	          return null;
272	        }
273	        const frames = (vanillaNpcFrames as Record<string, number>)[key] ?? 1;
274	        const fh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
275	        const meta: VanillaNpcMeta = { sheet, frameW: img.width, frameH: fh, count: frames };
276	        this.lazyNpcMeta.set(key, meta);
277	        m = meta;
278	      }
279	    }
280	    const img = this.vimages.get(m.sheet);
281	    if (!img) return null;
282	    const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(m.count - 1, frameIdx));
283	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: idx * m.frameH, sw: m.frameW, sh: m.frameH };
284	  }
285	
286	  /** 原版 tile/item 英文名（全量表，未白名单的也有） */
287	  vTileName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.tileNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
288	  vItemName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.itemNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
289	
290	  vnpcMeta(npcId: number): VanillaNpcMeta | null {
291	    return this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)] ?? null;
292	  }
293	
294	  /** 原版杂项单图（呼吸气泡等） */
295	  vmisc(path: string): DrawRect | null {
296	    const img = this.vimages.get(path);
297	    if (!img) return null;
298	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
299	  }
300	
301	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/ 命名空间）。name 为白名单键，如 'UI_PanelBackground'。
302	   *  按需懒加载(消费方每帧重查,未就绪返回 null 自兜底) */
303	  vui(name: string): DrawRect | null {
304	    const path = this.uiFiles[name];
305	    if (!path) return null;
306	    const img = this.ensureUiImage(path);
307	    if (!img) return null;
308	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
309	  }
310	
311	  private _uiPending = new Set<string>();
312	  private ensureUiImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
313	    const hit = this.uiimages.get(file);
314	    if (hit) return hit;
315	    if (this._uiPending.has(file)) return null;
316	    this._uiPending.add(file);
317	    const im = new Image();
318	    im.onload = () => { this.uiimages.set(file, im); this._uiPending.delete(file); };
319	    im.onerror = () => { this._uiPending.delete(file); };
320	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
321	    return null;
322	  }
323	
324	  /** 原版物品图标。构建期已 shelf-pack 进少数 Item_Atlas_k.png 图集(ix/iy/iw/ih
325	   *  子矩形,见 scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs);旧单体条目(无矩形字段)回退整图语义。
326	   *  未加载时触发后台懒加载并返回 null(下帧生效) */
327	  vicon(itemId: number): DrawRect | null {
328	    const m = this.vanilla.items[String(itemId)];
329	    if (!m) return null;
330	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.icon);
331	    if (!img) return null;
332	    if (m.ix !== undefined && m.iw !== undefined && m.ih !== undefined) {
333	      return { img, sx: m.ix, sy: m.iy ?? 0, sw: m.iw, sh: m.ih };
334	    }
335	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
336	  }
337	
338	  private _iconPending = new Set<string>();
339	  private _worldPreloaded = false;
340	  /** 懒加载完成回调(Game 注册 → ChunkCache 全量标脏:晚到的表重新烘焙
341	   *  已缓存的 chunk,否则 fallback 会永久烤进 canvas) */
342	  onVImageLoaded: ((file: string) => void) | null = null;
343	
344	  /** 预载文件清单(去重+decode)。tile/wall/NPC/misc 表与图标的统一底层 */
345	  async preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
346	    const list = [...new Set(files)];
347	    let done = 0;
348	    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
349	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
350	      const img = new Image();
351	      img.onload = () => {
352	        this.vimages.set(f, img);
353	        // decode():预载阶段完成解码,否则 Chrome 延迟到首次 draw 才解码,
354	        // 大表(2000px 级)首帧仍会卡一拍——"预载"语义要求字节+解码双就绪
355	        img.decode().then(() => { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve(); }, () => resolve());
356	        this.onVImageLoaded?.(f);
357	      };
358	      img.onerror = () => resolve();
359	      img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
360	    })));
361	  }
362	
363	  /** 按图块 sheet id + 墙 id 预载对应贴图表。
364	   *  Game 用出生点区域类型扫描调用——只载画面涉及的表(出生点半径内实测仅
365	   *  22/378 张图块表),而不是全量 ~750 张(~250MB 解码) */
366	  preloadTileSheetsFor(tileSheets: Iterable<number>, wallIds: Iterable<number>): Promise<void> {
367	    const files = new Set<string>();
368	    for (const id of tileSheets) {
369	      const m = this.vanilla.tiles[String(id)];
370	      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
371	    }
372	    for (const id of wallIds) {
373	      const m = this.vanilla.walls[String(id)];
374	      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
375	    }
376	    return this.preloadFiles(files);
377	  }
378	
379	  /** 预载常驻杂项(树冠/液体/瀑布/电路)+ NPC 表(小动物)——出生点必有,量小全载 */
380	  preloadMiscAndNpcs(): Promise<void> {
381	    return this.preloadFiles([
382	      ...VANILLA_MISC,
383	      ...Object.values(this.vanilla.npcs ?? {}).map((n) => n.sheet),
384	    ]);
385	  }
386	
387	  /** 预载世界渲染所需原版表(全量,~750 张)。仅调试/兜底用;正常路径走
388	   *  preloadTileSheetsFor + preloadMiscAndNpcs 的出生点子集 */
389	  async preloadVanillaWorld(): Promise<void> {
390	    if (this._worldPreloaded) return;
391	    this._worldPreloaded = true;
392	    await Promise.all([
393	      this.preloadTileSheetsFor(
394	        Object.keys(this.vanilla.tiles).map(Number),
395	        Object.keys(this.vanilla.walls).map(Number),
396	      ),
397	      this.preloadMiscAndNpcs(),
398	    ]);
399	  }
400	  /** 按需加载 vanilla 单图(去重;失败静默)。命中返回元素,否则 null。
401	   *  渲染路径一律用此入口(经 vimg 别名)——直接 vimages.get 会绕过懒加载与
402	   *  onVImageLoaded 重烘焙钩子(棕榈树干传送消失的根因) */
403	  ensureVImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
404	    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
405	    if (hit) return hit;
406	    if (this._iconPending.has(file)) return null;
407	    this._iconPending.add(file);
408	    const im = new Image();
409	    im.onload = () => {
410	      this.vimages.set(file, im);
411	      this._iconPending.delete(file);
412	      this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
413	    };
414	    im.onerror = () => { this._iconPending.delete(file); };
415	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
416	    return null;
417	  }
418	  /** 后台预取全部物品图标(保留兼容入口;预载流程请用 awaited 的 preloadIcons) */
419	  prefetchIcons(): void {
420	    void this.preloadIcons();
421	  }
422	
423	  private _iconsPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
424	  /** 预载全部物品图标素材。图集化后清单 = 去重后的 ~3 张 Item_Atlas_k.png
425	   *  (此前 6059 张单体逐张请求);缓存 Promise——并发 await 的调用者
426	   *  都会等到同一批加载完成(此前旗标早退会让第二个调用者拿到假完成) */
427	  preloadIcons(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
428	    if (this._iconsPromise) return this._iconsPromise;
429	    const icons = [...new Set(Object.values(this.vanilla.items).map((m) => m.icon))];
430	    let done = 0;
431	    this._iconsPromise = Promise.all(icons.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
432	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); return resolve(); }
433	      const im = new Image();
434	      im.onload = () => { this.vimages.set(f, im); done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); resolve(); };
435	      im.onerror = () => resolve();
436	      im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
437	    }))).then(() => undefined);
438	    return this._iconsPromise;
439	  }
440	
441	  /** 预载 UI 贴图按 key 前缀(如 ['Player_'] = 纸娃娃身体/发型,545 张)。 */
442	  async preloadUiPrefix(prefixes: string[], onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
443	    const files = Object.entries(this.uiFiles)
444	      .filter(([k]) => prefixes.some((p) => k.startsWith(p)))
445	      .map(([, f]) => f);
446	    return this.preloadUiFiles(files, onProgress);
447	  }
448	
449	  /** 按 UI 文件路径预载(装备中的具体 Armor 表;decode 保证首帧无解码卡顿) */
450	  async preloadUiFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
451	    const list = [...new Set(files)];
452	    let done = 0;
453	    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
454	      if (this.uiimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
455	      const im = new Image();
456	      im.onload = () => {
457	        this.uiimages.set(f, im);
458	        im.decode().then(() => { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve(); }, () => resolve());
459	      };
460	      im.onerror = () => resolve();
461	      im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
462	    })));
463	  }
464	
465	  has(file: string): boolean {
466	    return this.images.has(file);
467	  }
468	
469	  /** 取精灵绘制矩形（Unity y 翻转已处理）。找不到返回 null。 */
470	  rect(file: string, sprite: string): DrawRect | null {
471	    const img = this.images.get(file);
472	    const entry = this.data.files[file];
473	    if (!img || !entry) return null;
474	    const s = entry.sprites.find((x) => x.name === sprite);
475	    if (!s) return null;
476	    return { img, sx: s.x, sy: img.height - s.y - s.h, sw: s.w, sh: s.h };
477	  }
478	
479	  animFrames(anim: string): DrawRect[] {
480	    const frames = this.resources.anims[anim] ?? [];
481	    return frames.map((f) => this.rect(f.file, f.sprite)).filter(Boolean) as DrawRect[];
482	  }
483	}

485	// ---- 物品图标：一律原版 Item_N（旧 Maples UI/ 图标层已整体移除） ----
486	import { TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
487	
488	/** DrawAnimationVertical(int.MaxValue, 3) 的静态等价(IsFood 全表) */
489	const FOOD = { dur: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, n: 3, static: true };
490	const FOOD_IDS: number[] = [
491	  353, 357, 967, 969, 1787, 1911, 1912, 1919, 1920, 2266, 2267, 2268, 
492	  2425, 2426, 2427, 3195, 3532, 4009, 4010, 4011, 4012, 4013, 4014, 4015, 
493	  4016, 4017, 4018, 4019, 4020, 4021, 4022, 4023, 4024, 4025, 4026, 4027, 
494	  4028, 4029, 4030, 4031, 4032, 4033, 4034, 4035, 4036, 4037, 4282, 4283, 
495	  4284, 4285, 4286, 4287, 4288, 4289, 4290, 4291, 4292, 4293, 4294, 4295, 
496	  4296, 4297, 4403, 4411, 4614, 4615, 4616, 4617, 4618, 4619, 4620, 4621, 
497	  4622, 4623, 4624, 4625, 5009, 5041, 5042, 5092, 5093, 5275, 5277, 5278, 
498	  5537, 5645, 
499	];
500	
501	/**
502	 * 物品贴图动画注册表(Main.InitializeItemAnimations :3685-3722 1:1)。
503	 * 食物族:ItemID.Sets.IsFood(:258,86 项)全部为竖 3 帧条,原版注册
504	 * DrawAnimationVertical(int.MaxValue,3) = 恒帧 0(顶部一帧);本表以 FOOD
505	 * 静态条目等价表达(切片帧高 = 总高/3)。
506	 * 物品源图是竖排多帧条(如坠星 Item_75=22×208),不切片会整条入画。
507	 * dur=tick/帧;pingPong=往返;static=true=恒帧 0(IsFood 的 int.MaxValue
508	 * 静态三帧与 NotActuallyAnimating 族)。
509	 */
510	export const ITEM_ANIMATION: Record<number, { dur: number; n: number; pingPong?: boolean; static?: boolean }> = {
511	  3581: { dur: 4, n: 4 },
512	  3580: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
513	  75: { dur: 5, n: 8, pingPong: true },    // 坠星 8 帧 PingPong 旋转
514	  575: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 547: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 520: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
515	  548: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 521: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 549: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
516	  3453: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 3454: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 3455: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
517	  4068: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },   // NotActuallyAnimating(:3701-3709)
518	  4069: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },
519	  4070: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },
520	  5644: { dur: 7, n: 9 },                 // ScryingOrb(专属着色器,垂直循环近似)
521	  // ---- 食物族(IsFood :258):竖 3 帧条,原版 int.MaxValue 恒帧 0 ----
522	  ...Object.fromEntries(FOOD_IDS.map((v) => [v, FOOD])),
523	};
524	
525	
526	/** 物品动画当前帧(DrawAnimationVertical.GetFrame 语义;tick=游戏 tick 或等价毫秒换算) */
527	export function itemAnimFrame(vid: number, tick: number): number {
528	  const a = ITEM_ANIMATION[vid];
529	  if (!a || a.static) return 0;
530	  const span = a.pingPong ? a.n * 2 - 2 : a.n;
531	  const idx = Math.floor(tick / a.dur) % span;
532	  return a.pingPong && idx >= a.n ? span - idx : idx;
533	}
534	
535	/** 把整条 DrawRect 按帧切片(vid 无动画原样返回) */
536	export function sliceItemAnimFrame(vid: number, ar: DrawRect, tick: number): DrawRect {
537	  const a = ITEM_ANIMATION[vid];
538	  if (!a || a.n <= 1) return ar;
539	  const fh = ar.sh / a.n;
540	  const f = Math.min(a.n - 1, itemAnimFrame(vid, tick));
541	  return { img: ar.img, sx: ar.sx, sy: ar.sy + Math.round(fh * f), sw: ar.sw, sh: Math.round(fh) };
542	}
543	
544	export function atlasIconForKey(atlas: SpriteAtlas, key: string): DrawRect | null {
545	  const vid = VANILLA_ITEM_ICON_MAP[key];
546	  if (vid !== undefined) return atlas.vicon(vid);
547	  // 阶段 5:vi_<id>_* 全量物品回退原版图标
548	  if (key.startsWith('vi_')) {
549	    const id = parseInt(key.slice(3), 10);
550	    if (Number.isFinite(id)) return atlas.vicon(id);
551	  }
552	  // 阶段 5:place_v_* 放置物品回退用图块贴图首帧作图标
553	  if (key.startsWith('place_v_')) {
554	    const tk = key.slice('place_'.length);
555	    const tid = TILE_BY_KEY[tk];
556	    if (tid !== undefined) {
557	      const td = TILE_DEFS[tid];
558	      // vframeAt 而非 vframe:窄条表(如压板 135 的 16×200 竖条)cols=0 会让 vframe 判越界
559	      if (td?.vanilla) return atlas.vframeAt(td.vanilla.sheet, 0, 0) ?? atlas.vframe(td.vanilla.sheet, 0, 0);
560	    }
561	  }
562	  return null;
563	}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T05:53:49.176Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Important findings on the atlas:

1. `vimages` Map is unbounded — every loaded PNG is cached forever. With ~5800 PNGs, full preloadVanillaWorld loads ~750 tile sheets + 368 walls = potentially 250MB decoded (the code comment says "~250MB 解码" for full preload). And `preloadIcons()` loads all Item_Atlas (~3 files now). So the "1万张PNG" concern: it's now atlased to ~3 files, but `vimages` never evicts. If full preload happens (debug/fallback path `preloadVanillaWorld`), that's ~250MB+ retained. Unbounded cache — by design, no eviction. Medium severity: memory floor high on low-end, no leak per se (bounded by asset count), but `lazyNpcMeta` and `vimages` grow over a session.

2. `ensureVImage` on-miss → network fetch + `onVImageLoaded` → `chunks.invalidateAll()` for every Tiles_/Wall_ file. Since exploration triggers new sheets, each arrival invalidates ALL cached chunks (384 max) → dirtyQueue grows to hundreds, flushDirty 4/frame/6ms. With many arrivals over a short window, this creates sustained bake churn. Confirmed chronic hitch source. Also `invalidateAll` + `markDirty` both use `dirtyQueue.includes` → O(n²).

Wait, actually — is it true that a new Tiles_ file arriving mid-game means previously baked chunks used fallback? Chunks containing that sheet would have baked fallback (magenta?) and need rebuild. invalidateAll is the blunt fix — rebuild ALL chunks even those unaffected. The cheap fix: track which sheets each chunk used (they do have animChunksBySheet only for animated ones) and invalidate only chunks referencing the late sheet.

3. `img.decode()` is called in preloadFiles BEFORE `onVImageLoaded(f)` (line 356 fires onVImageLoaded synchronously in onload before decode resolves). So onVImageLoaded → invalidateAll happens before decode completes → chunk bake on next frame may still hit undecoded image → double invalidation possible. Minor.

Also note line 356: `this.onVImageLoaded?.(f)` is inside onload for preloadFiles, and `img.decode().then(...)` — the resolve happens after decode, good.

But in `ensureVImage` (line 409-413), no decode() call → lazy-loaded images decode on first draw → first-draw hitch! The comment in preloadFiles explains Chrome defers decoding until first draw. So any lazy-loaded vimage (NPC sheets via vnpc, icons via vicon, UI images via vui) will hitch on first draw. That's the "贴图首次 decode" hitch the user asked about. Confirmed: `ensureVImage` lacks `im.decode()`. Severity: 中 (one-off hitches when new enemy types/projectiles appear).

Now let me look at the other files: BiomeBackground tintCache/drawTiledTinted, Renderer caches (frameCache), Portal.frameCache, Arrow.projFrameImg, Audio/Sfx, Lang warn set, LiquidSim checking/skip, Wiring cooldowns, LightingEngine, SaveClient, MobileControls, ClientNet, minimap fullMap.

Let me read several in parallel.
```

</details>

434	  /** 带色调平铺绘制（tint 缓存按 (tex,tint) 键，避免每帧逐像素） */
435	  private tintCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();
436	  private drawTiledTinted(
437	    ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, im: HTMLImageElement,
438	    tr: number, tg: number, tb: number, dx: number, dy: number, dw: number, dh: number,
439	  ) {
440	    const key = `${im.src}|${tr.toFixed(2)},${tg.toFixed(2)},${tb.toFixed(2)}`;
441	    let c = this.tintCache.get(key);
442	    if (!c) {
443	      c = document.createElement('canvas');
444	      c.width = im.naturalWidth; c.height = im.naturalHeight;
445	      const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
446	      cx.drawImage(im, 0, 0);
447	      cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
448	      cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${Math.round(tr * 255)},${Math.round(tg * 255)},${Math.round(tb * 255)})`;
449	      cx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
450	      cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
451	      cx.drawImage(im, 0, 0);
452	      this.tintCache.set(key, c);
453	      if (this.tintCache.size > 64) this.tintCache.clear(); // 简单防膨胀
454	    }
455	    ctx.drawImage(c, dx, dy, dw, dh);
456	  }

  constructor(x: number, y: number, angle: number, form: number) {
    super();
    this.x = x - 5;   // 602 碰撞盒 10×10（SetDefaults），实体锚点存中心语义 → 左上
    this.y = y - 5;
    this.w = 10; this.h = 10;
    this.angle = angle;
    this.form = form;
  }

  get centerX(): number { return this.x + 5; }
  get centerY(): number { return this.y + 5; }

  /** 门线两端（GetPortalEdges：中心 ± 22×angle 向量） */
  edges(): [number, number, number, number] {
    const dx = Math.cos(this.angle) * 22, dy = Math.sin(this.angle) * 22;
    return [this.centerX - dx, this.centerY - dy, this.centerX + dx, this.centerY + dy];
  }

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
    if (this.cooldown > 0) this.cooldown--;
    this.animT++;
    if (this.animT >= 4) { this.animT = 0; this.anim = (this.anim + 1) % 10; } // 原版 frameCounter 4tick 步进
    // 穿越判定（TryGoingThroughPortals :105-215）
    const p = game.player;
    if (p.dead || this.cooldown > 0) return;
    const [x1, y1, x2, y2] = this.edges();
    const pb = { x: p.x + p.vx, y: p.y + p.vy, w: p.w, h: p.h };
    if (!segHitsRect(x1, y1, x2, y2, pb.x - 2, pb.y - 2, pb.w + 4, pb.h + 4)) return;
    const other = portalRegistry().find((o) => o !== this && !o.dead && o.owner === this.owner && o.form !== this.form);
    if (!other) return;
    const st = game.world.store;
    // 出口点（GetPortalOutingPoint :372-394，返回实体左上落点）+ 沿出口法线推开原距离（:133-136）
    const [ox, oy, bx, by] = portalOutingPoint(p.w, p.h, other.centerX, other.centerY, other.angle);
    const dist = Math.hypot(p.cx - this.centerX, p.cy - this.centerY);
    const bl = Math.hypot(bx, by) || 1;
    const tx = ox + (bx / bl) * dist, ty = oy + (by / bl) * dist;
    // 四向 16px 扫掠净空（:138-161：任一方向受阻则放弃本次穿越）
    for (const [dx, dy] of [[16, 0], [-16, 0], [0, 16], [0, -16]] as const) {
      if (!sweepFree(st, tx, ty, dx, dy, p.w, p.h)) return;
    }
    // 速度改向（:163-186）：保速率，指向出口法线；零速给 0.1 最小推力
    let speed = Math.hypot(p.vx, p.vy);
    if (speed < 0.1) speed = 0.1;
    let dirX = bx / bl, dirY = by / bl;
    if (bx === 0 && by === 0) { dirX = p.facing || 1; dirY = 0; }
    p.vx = dirX * speed;
    p.vy = dirY * speed;
    if (by === -1 && p.vy > -0.1) p.vy = -0.1;  // 顶部门最小上抛（:177-180）
    p.x = tx;
    p.y = ty;
    this.cooldown = 10;
    other.cooldown = 10;
  }

  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_602.png');
    const [cr, cg, cb] = portalColor(this.form);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(this.centerX, this.centerY);
    ctx.rotate(this.angle);
    if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
      const frame = Portal.tintedFrame(img, this.anim, cr, cg, cb);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.95;
      ctx.drawImage(frame, -9, -25);
    } else {
      // 贴图未就位回退：门色椭圆环（原版门基色 A=66/255，:407）
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.85;
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgb(${cr},${cg},${cb})`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 4;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 6, 22, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

  /** 帧着色缓存（multiply + destination-in 保形；10 帧 × 2 色） */
  private static tintedFrame(tex: HTMLImageElement, frame: number, r: number, g: number, b: number): HTMLCanvasElement {
    const key = `${tex.src}|${frame}|${r},${g},${b}`;
    const hit = Portal.frameCache.get(key);
    if (hit) return hit;
    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
    c.width = 18; c.height = 20;
    const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
    cx.drawImage(tex, 0, frame * 20, 18, 20, 0, 0, 18, 20);
    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
    cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
    cx.fillRect(0, 0, 18, 20);
    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
    cx.drawImage(tex, 0, frame * 20, 18, 20, 0, 0, 18, 20);
    Portal.frameCache.set(key, c);
    return c;
  }
}

/** 门注册表（props 桶同一实例；AddPortal 的 RemoveMyOldPortal/相交清理在此做） */
const registry: Portal[] = [];
export function portalRegistry(): Portal[] { return registry; }
export function clearPortals(): void { for (const p of registry) p.dead = true; registry.length = 0; }

/** 线段 × 矩形（Liang-Barsky 裁剪；Collision.CheckAABBvLineCollision 近似） */
function segHitsRect(x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number, rx: number, ry: number, rw: number, rh: number): boolean {
  let t0 = 0, t1 = 1;
  const dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1;
  const clip = (p: number, q: number): boolean => {
    if (p === 0) return q >= 0;
    const r = q / p;
    if (p < 0) { if (r > t1) return false; if (r > t0) t0 = r; }
    else { if (r < t0) return false; if (r < t1) t1 = r; }
    return true;
  };
  return clip(-dx, x1 - rx) && clip(dx, rx + rw - x1) && clip(-dy, y1 - ry) && clip(dy, ry + rh - y1);
}

/** 矩形区域无实心块 */
function boxFree(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number): boolean {
  const x0 = Math.floor(x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((x + w - 0.01) / TILE);

358	    const num3 = -(camTopY - 300) / (world.groundLevel * 16);
359	    const drawLayer = (l: LayerDef, alpha: number) => {
360	      if (alpha <= 0.01 || l.tex < 0) return;
361	      const im = this.img(l.tex);
362	      if (!im || !im.complete || im.naturalWidth === 0) return;
363	      const wScaled = im.naturalWidth * l.scale;
364	      const startX = -(((cam.x * l.parallax) % wScaled) + wScaled) % wScaled - wScaled / 2;
365	      const loops = Math.ceil(viewW / wScaled) + 2;
366	      const topY = num3 * l.topA + l.topB; // 屏幕空间 y（vanilla bgTopY 语义）
367	      ctx.save();
368	      ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
369	      const [tr, tg, tb] = tint;
370	      // ★+1px 保险重叠:浮点视差位置(startX 取模 cam.x*parallax)+非整数缩放
371	      // (naturalWidth×1.25)下,相邻背景图独立光栅化在接缝处留 1px 缺口(发丝缝),
372	      // 双线性平滑还会把边缘混透明放大缝。外扩 1px 让邻图覆盖接缝
373	      const dw = wScaled + 1;
374	      for (let i = 0; i < loops; i++) {
375	        if (tr >= 0.999 && tg >= 0.999 && tb >= 0.999) {
376	          ctx.drawImage(im, startX + i * wScaled, topY, dw, im.naturalHeight * l.scale);
377	        } else {
378	          // 先画原图再叠 tint（保持边缘 alpha）：用 offscreen 缓存避免每帧 getImageData
379	          this.drawTiledTinted(ctx, im, tr, tg, tb, startX + i * wScaled, topY, dw, im.naturalHeight * l.scale);
380	        }
381	      }
382	      ctx.restore();
383	    };
384	    // 远山层（bgAlphaFarBackLayer；parallax 0.15/scale 1，:59240）

```

1	// 原版 BGM 系统（LegacyAudioSystem.cs 交叉淡化语义 1:1）：
2	// WebAudio AudioBufferSourceNode 无缝循环（loop=true，解码后内存播放，零接缝）——
3	// 修复 <audio loop> 播 mp3 的循环点卡顿 + 切曲冷启动顿挫（原版 XACT 同为解码循环）。
4	// musicFade[id] ±0.005/帧 逼近目标曲（:281 UpdateCommonTrack / :309 TowardStopping）。
5	// audios/music/Music_<id>.mp3（tools/xwb-extract.mjs 提取）。
6	import { MUSIC } from '../data/Music';
7	
8	const TITLE_ID = MUSIC.TitleIntro; // 50
9	
10	interface Track {
11	  src: AudioBufferSourceNode;
12	  gain: GainNode;
13	  fade: number;   // 0..1（≈原版 Main.musicFade[id]）
14	}
15	
16	export class AudioSystem {
17	  private ac: AudioContext | null = null;
18	  private pool = new Map<number, Track>();
19	  private buffers = new Map<number, AudioBuffer>();
20	  private decoding = new Set<number>();
21	  /** 目标曲目（≈原版 Main.curMusic；0 = 静音） */
22	  curMusic = 0;
23	  muted = false;
24	  /** 音乐音量 0..1（options.musicVol，M6 设置页） */
25	  volume = 0.35;
26	  private rafId = 0;
27	  private lastTs = 0;
28	
29	  constructor() {
30	    this.startLoop();
31	  }
32	
33	  private audio(): AudioContext | null {
34	    if (!this.ac) {
35	      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
36	      if (!AC) return null;
37	      this.ac = new AC();
38	    }
39	    if (this.ac.state === 'suspended') this.ac.resume().catch(() => { /* 手势前恢复被拒，播放时重试 */ });
40	    return this.ac;
41	  }
42	
43	  /** 解码缓存（每曲一次；解码失败标记 null 防重复请求） */
44	  private async buffer(id: number): Promise<AudioBuffer | null> {
45	    const hit = this.buffers.get(id);
46	    if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
47	    if (this.decoding.has(id)) return null; // 解码中：本帧跳过，就绪后 track() 会补建
48	    this.decoding.add(id);
49	    try {
50	      const r = await fetch(`audios/music/Music_${id}.mp3`);
51	      if (!r.ok) throw new Error(String(r.status));
52	      const buf = await this.ac!.decodeAudioData(await r.arrayBuffer());
53	      this.buffers.set(id, buf);
54	      return buf;
55	    } catch {
56	      this.buffers.set(id, null as unknown as AudioBuffer); // 永久失败标记
57	      return null;
58	    } finally {
59	      this.decoding.delete(id);
60	    }
61	  }
62	
63	  /** 建立播放源（目标曲就绪后调用；重复调用幂等） */
64	  private track(id: number) {
65	    const ac = this.audio();
66	    if (!ac) return;
67	    if (this.pool.has(id)) return;
68	    const buf = this.buffers.get(id);
69	    if (!buf) return; // 未就绪：ensureTrack 的解码回调里补建
70	    const src = ac.createBufferSource();
71	    src.buffer = buf;
72	    src.loop = true;
73	    const gain = ac.createGain();
74	    gain.gain.value = 0;
75	    src.connect(gain).connect(ac.destination);
76	    src.start();
77	    this.pool.set(id, { src, gain, fade: 0 });
78	  }
79	
80	  /** rAF 驱动淡化（原版按游戏帧 ±0.005，这里按 60fps 归一时间步进） */
81	  private startLoop() {
82	    if (this.rafId) return;
83	    const step = (ts: number) => {
84	      const dt = this.lastTs ? Math.min(100, ts - this.lastTs) : 16.7;
85	      this.lastTs = ts;
86	      const frames = dt / (1000 / 60);
87	      this.tickFade(frames * 0.005);
88	      this.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(step);
89	    };
90	    this.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(step);
91	  }
92	
93	  /** 步进淡化量 d（0.005/帧）：目标曲 fade+=d 至 1，其余 -=d 至 0 并停播 */
94	  private tickFade(d: number) {
95	    if (this.muted) {
96	      for (const [id, t] of this.pool) {
97	        if (t.fade > 0) { t.fade = 0; t.gain.gain.value = 0; }
98	      }
99	      return;
100	    }
101	    for (const [id, t] of this.pool) {
102	      const target = id === this.curMusic ? 1 : 0;
103	      if (t.fade < target) {
104	        t.fade = Math.min(target, t.fade + d);
105	        // AudioContext 挂起态（浏览器手势前）：gain 无声但源持续跑，恢复后即出声
106	      } else if (t.fade > target) {
107	        t.fade = Math.max(target, t.fade - d);
108	        if (t.fade === 0) {
109	          t.src.stop();
110	          this.pool.delete(id);
111	          continue;
112	        }
113	      }
114	      t.gain.gain.value = Math.min(1, t.fade) * this.volume;
115	    }
116	  }
117	
118	  setVolume(v: number) {
119	    this.volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
120	  }
121	
122	  /** 切换目标曲目（≈原版 curMusic 赋值；实际音量由淡化循环渐变） */
123	  playMusic(id: number) {
124	    if (this.muted) return;
125	    if (this.curMusic === id) return;
126	    this.curMusic = id;
127	    if (id === 0) return;
128	    const ac = this.audio();
129	    if (!ac) return;
130	    // 缓冲未就绪：解码完成后补建源（此时才真正起播，无冷启动顿挫）
131	    if (!this.buffers.has(id)) {
132	      void this.buffer(id).then((buf) => { if (buf && this.curMusic === id) this.track(id); });
133	    } else {
134	      this.track(id);
135	    }
136	  }
137	
138	  /** 兼容旧调用：'title' = 原版标题曲；'main' = 交由环境选曲接管（此处仅解除标题曲） */
139	  play(which: 'title' | 'main') {
140	    if (this.muted) return;
141	    if (which === 'title') this.playMusic(TITLE_ID);
142	    else if (this.curMusic === TITLE_ID) this.playMusic(0);
143	  }
144	
145	  /** 旧昼夜变奏钩子：曲目切换已由选曲链接管（昼/夜曲不同 id），无操作 */
146	  setDayNight(_isDay: boolean) { /* no-op */ }
147	
148	  stop() {
149	    this.curMusic = 0;
150	  }
151	
152	  toggleMute(): boolean {
153	    this.muted = !this.muted;
154	    if (this.muted) this.stop();
155	    return this.muted;
156	  }
157	}
158

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T05:54:02.977Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Audio.ts (BGM): `buffers` Map grows unbounded — each music track decoded stays in memory forever. Number of music tracks ~90? Each mp3 decoded to PCM ~ maybe 2-5MB each. 90 tracks × 3MB = 270MB potential if all played in a session. Realistically a long session cycles through many tracks (day/night/boss/biome) → could be 30-50 tracks = 100MB+. Unbounded, no eviction. Severity: 中 (memory growth over long session). Also `pool` tracks are stopped and deleted when fade hits 0 — good.

Also note: `tickFade` rAF loop runs always even when muted/paused — trivial.

The `buffer()` fetch + decodeAudioData on main thread — decodeAudioData runs off-main-thread in modern browsers. Fetch + arrayBuffer allocation. On music switch, could hitch slightly. It's awaited async so no blocking, but decodeAudioData allocates big buffers. Minor.

Now Sfx.ts — per-wav buffers. Let me read it, plus Lang.ts/LanguageManager.ts.
```
