102	export class Sfx {
103	  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
104	  private buffers = new Map<string, AudioBuffer>();
105	  /** 在途解码中的文件（防重复 fetch） */
106	  private pending = new Set<string>();
107	  /** 取回失败/不存在的文件（负缓存，防 404 每次重试） */
108	  private failed = new Set<string>();
109	  muted = false;
110	  /** 主音量 0..1（M6 设置页） */
111	  master = 1;
112	
113	  /** 按需取缓冲：未命中时后台 fetch+decode，同步返回已就位缓冲或 null */
114	  private ensureBuffer(file: string): AudioBuffer | null {
115	    const buf = this.buffers.get(file);
116	    if (buf) return buf;
117	    if (this.pending.has(file) || this.failed.has(file)) return null;
118	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
119	    if (!ctx) return null;
120	    this.pending.add(file);
121	    fetch(`sounds/${file}.wav`)
122	      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
123	      .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
124	      .then((decoded) => {
125	        if (decoded) this.buffers.set(file, decoded);
126	        else this.failed.add(file);
127	      })
128	      .catch(() => { this.failed.add(file); })
129	      .finally(() => { this.pending.delete(file); });
130	    return null;
131	  }
132	
133	  /** 定向预热（不阻塞）：把指定 wav 后台取齐，播放时即命中 */
134	  preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>) {
135	    for (const f of files) this.ensureBuffer(f);
136	  }
137	
138	  /** 按逻辑名预热（WAV_MAP 展开到具体文件） */
139	  preloadNames(names: SfxName[]) {
140	    for (const n of names) for (const f of WAV_MAP[n] ?? []) this.ensureBuffer(f);
141	  }
142	
143	  // ---- 雨声层：原版 RainAmbience（Music_28.mp3，XACT Wave Bank 同源提取）循环 ----
144	  private rainGain: GainNode | null = null;
145	  private rainSrc: AudioBufferSourceNode | null = null;
146	  private rainLoaded = false;
147	  /** 设置雨声强度 0-1（每帧调用；0 时静音不拆节点——重启有冷启动开销） */
148	  setRain(vol: number) {
149	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
150	    if (!ctx) return;
151	    if (!this.rainGain) {
152	      const g = ctx.createGain();
153	      g.gain.value = 0;
154	      g.connect(ctx.destination);
155	      this.rainGain = g;
156	      // 原版雨环境音轨（RainAmbience）懒加载循环；加载失败静默（雨声可选）
157	      fetch('audios/music/Music_28.mp3')
158	        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
159	        .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
160	        .then((buf) => {
161	          if (!buf) return;
162	          const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
163	          src.buffer = buf;
164	          src.loop = true;
165	          src.connect(this.rainGain!);
166	          src.start();
167	          this.rainSrc = src;
168	          this.rainLoaded = true;
169	        })
170	        .catch(() => {});
171	    }
172	    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vol)) * this.master * 0.6;
173	    this.rainGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(v, ctx.currentTime, 0.4);
174	  }
175	
176	  /** 世界声距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:160 SoundAttenuationDistance = 2500px） */
177	  static readonly ATTEN = 2500;
178	  /** 监听器（原版 Main.Camera.Center，相机中心非玩家）；Game 每帧更新。未进世界=(0,0) */
179	  private lx = 0; private ly = 0;
180	  setListener(x: number, y: number) { this.lx = x; this.ly = y; }
181	  /** 原版距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:381-401）：dist ≥2500 → 不播；
182	   *  vol × (1 - dist/2500)。不传坐标（x=-1 语义）→ 全量（UI/玩家自身动作声） */
183	  private atten(vol: number, x?: number, y?: number): number {
184	    if (x == null || y == null) return vol;
185	    const d = Math.hypot(x - this.lx, y - this.ly);
186	    if (d >= Sfx.ATTEN) return 0;
187	    return vol * (1 - d / Sfx.ATTEN);
188	  }
189	
190	  private ensure(silent = false): AudioContext | null {
191	    if (this.muted && !silent) return null;
192	    if (!this.ctx) {
193	      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
194	      if (!AC) return null;
195	      this.ctx = new AC();
196	    }
197	    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
198	    return this.ctx;
199	  }
200	
201	  /** 播放原版 wav（随机变体 + 轻微音高扰动）；vol 0-1 */
202	  playWav(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
203	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
204	    if (!list || !list.length) return false;
205	    return this.playWavFile(list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)], vol, x, y);
206	  }
207	
208	  /** 播放任意 wav 文件列表（随机选 + 音高扰动）；供怪物专属声效用 */
209	  playFiles(files: string[], vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
210	    if (!files.length) return false;
211	    return this.playWavFile(files[Math.floor(Math.random() * files.length)], vol, x, y);
212	  }
213	
214	  /** 按文件名直播（缓冲未就位时触发后台加载，本播返回 false 走合成兜底） */
215	  playWavFile(file: string, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
216	    const ctx = this.ensure();
217	    if (!ctx) return false;
218	    const v = this.atten(vol, x, y);
219	    if (v <= 0) return false;
220	    vol = v;
221	    const buf = this.ensureBuffer(file);
222	    if (!buf) return false;
223	    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
224	    src.buffer = buf;
225	    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
226	    const g = ctx.createGain();
227	    g.gain.value = Math.min(1, vol * this.master);
228	    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
229	    src.start();
230	    return true;
231	  }
232	
233	  play(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number) {
234	    // 优先原版素材，失败回退合成（master 在 playWavFile/osc 各自生效一次）。
235	    // 带坐标时衰减（atten），≤0 直接不播——合成回退也不触发
236	    if (x != null && y != null && this.atten(vol, x, y) <= 0) return;
237	    if (this.playWav(name, vol, x, y)) return;
238	    const ctx = this.ensure();
239	    if (!ctx) return;
240	    const t = ctx.currentTime;
241	    const osc = (type: OscillatorType, f0: number, f1: number, dur: number, vol: number, delay = 0) => {
242	      const o = ctx.createOscillator();
243	      const g = ctx.createGain();
244	      o.type = type;
245	      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t + delay);
246	      o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, f1), t + delay + dur);
247	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * this.master, t + delay);
248	      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + delay + dur);
249	      o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
250	      o.start(t + delay);
251	      o.stop(t + delay + dur);
252	    };
253	    switch (name) {
254	      case 'hit': osc('square', 320, 110, 0.09, 0.12); break;
255	      case 'chop': osc('triangle', 220, 80, 0.12, 0.16); break;
256	      case 'hurt': osc('sawtooth', 180, 60, 0.22, 0.14); break;
257	      case 'killed': osc('square', 260, 60, 0.25, 0.14); break;
258	      case 'pkilled': osc('sawtooth', 160, 40, 0.6, 0.18); break;
259	      case 'zombie': osc('sawtooth', 90, 55, 0.5, 0.1); break;
260	      case 'roar': osc('sawtooth', 70, 40, 0.9, 0.2); break;
261	      case 'pickup': osc('sine', 520, 990, 0.09, 0.1); osc('sine', 780, 1320, 0.08, 0.06, 0.05); break;
262	      case 'dig': osc('square', 140, 90, 0.05, 0.06); break;
263	      case 'tink': osc('sine', 900, 500, 0.06, 0.08); break;
264	      case 'place': osc('triangle', 260, 120, 0.08, 0.12); break;
265	      case 'drink': osc('sine', 300, 620, 0.06, 0.08); osc('sine', 420, 780, 0.06, 0.07, 0.07); osc('sine', 560, 940, 0.06, 0.06, 0.14); break;
266	      case 'splash': osc('sine', 700, 200, 0.18, 0.1); break;
267	      case 'summon': {
268	        // 召唤杖 UseSound(Item_44)近似:短促"噗"——高频下扫正弦 + 一撮噪声气声
269	        osc('sine', 900, 260, 0.16, 0.14);
270	        osc('triangle', 1400, 500, 0.08, 0.06);
271	        break;
272	      }
273	      case 'whipCrack': {
274	        // 鞭响(Item_152)近似:高频噪声急促炸开 + 快速衰减
275	        const wDur = 0.12, wn = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * wDur);
276	        const wBuf = ctx.createBuffer(1, wn, ctx.sampleRate);
277	        const wd = wBuf.getChannelData(0);
278	        for (let i = 0; i < wn; i++) wd[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / wn, 3);
279	        const wSrc = ctx.createBufferSource();
280	        wSrc.buffer = wBuf;
281	        const hp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
282	        hp.type = 'highpass';
283	        hp.frequency.value = 1200;
284	        const wg = ctx.createGain();
285	        wg.gain.value = 0.18 * this.master;
286	        wSrc.connect(hp).connect(wg).connect(ctx.destination);
287	        wSrc.start(t);
288	        break;
289	      }
290	      case 'explosion': {
291	        // 噪声爆发近似(原版 Item_14):一次性白噪 buffer + 低频 thump。
292	        // ★曾无此分支:按需加载首播(wav 还在 fetch)完全静音 = "炸弹没有音效"主因
293	        const dur = 0.5, n = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * dur);
294	        const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, n, ctx.sampleRate);
295	        const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
296	        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / n, 2.2);
297	        const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
298	        src.buffer = buf;
299	        const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
300	        lp.type = 'lowpass';
301	        lp.frequency.setValueAtTime(900, t);
302	        lp.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(120, t + dur);
303	        const g = ctx.createGain();
304	        g.gain.value = 0.5 * this.master;
305	        src.connect(lp).connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
306	        src.start(t);
307	        osc('sine', 90, 35, 0.35, 0.35); // 低频冲击
308	        break;
309	      }
310	      case 'coin': osc('sine', 990, 1400, 0.07, 0.08); osc('sine', 1320, 1760, 0.09, 0.06, 0.05); break;
311	      case 'door_open': osc('triangle', 180, 320, 0.12, 0.1); break;
312	      case 'door_close': osc('triangle', 320, 160, 0.1, 0.1); break;
313	      case 'shatter': osc('square', 1400, 300, 0.15, 0.1); break;
314	      case 'drown': osc('sine', 400, 90, 0.4, 0.1); break;
315	      case 'mirror': osc('sine', 700, 1900, 0.3, 0.06); osc('sine', 1400, 2800, 0.25, 0.04, 0.1); break;   // 魔镜闪烁上行
316	      case 'conch': osc('sine', 220, 330, 0.25, 0.08); osc('sine', 330, 440, 0.3, 0.06, 0.2); break;        // 海螺低鸣
317	    }
318	  }
319	}
320	

```

35	  valueToCoins(value: number): string {
36	    let rest = Math.max(0, Math.floor(value));

45	    if (silver > 0) text += `${silver} ${Lang.text('Currency.Silver').toLowerCase()} `;
46	    if (copper > 0) text += `${copper} ${Lang.text('Currency.Copper').toLowerCase()} `;

1	// 语言包运行时核心。1:1 对照 Terarria1456/Terraria.Localization/LanguageManager.cs。
2	//
3	// 与原版的差异(有意为之,构建管线 scripts/build-l10n.mjs 已承担):
4	// - 原版 ReloadLanguage(L102)的"值重置为 key → 加载 en-US → 加载目标语言"回退,
5	//   在构建期完成(目标语言包已按 en 基线 overlay 缺失键),运行时单文件单请求。
6	// - {$Key} 跨键复制(L158)与 {?Cond} 条件标记也已构建期处理。
7	// - 本文件保留 loadPackJson 顺序加载语义(测试可连续注入模拟原版双载)。
8	
9	import { DEFAULT_CULTURE_NAME, GameCulture, normalizeLang } from './GameCulture';
10	
11	type Pack = Record<string, Record<string, string>>; // { [category]: { [key]: value } }
12	
13	export interface LangCultureInfo {
14	  name: string;
15	  legacyId: number;
16	  displayNameKey: string;
17	}
18	
19	/** 语言包数据表 + 类别索引(共用 full key 字符串引用,不复制内存) */
20	interface LoadedPack {
21	  texts: Map<string, string>;                  // full key = category + '.' + key
22	  groupedKeys: Map<string, string[]>;          // category → full keys(保插入序)
23	  variations: Map<string, Map<string, string>>; // base key → { variant: value }(LanguageManager.cs:300)
24	}
25	
26	const MAX_PACK_CACHE = 2; // LRU 上限(性能审计:语言包 ~1MB/份,不无限缓存)
27	
28	function buildTables(pack: Pack): LoadedPack {
29	  const texts = new Map<string, string>();
30	  const groupedKeys = new Map<string, string[]>();
31	  const variations = new Map<string, Map<string, string>>();
32	  for (const [cat, entries] of Object.entries(pack)) {
33	    const list: string[] = [];
34	    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(entries)) {
35	      const fullKey = `${cat}.${key}`;
36	      // $ 变体键(如 ItemName.Chair$Gender)入变体表,不进正文(LanguageManager.cs:303-317)
37	      const dollar = key.indexOf('$');
38	      if (dollar > 0) {
39	        const base = `${cat}.${key.slice(0, dollar)}`;
40	        let variants = variations.get(base);
41	        if (!variants) { variants = new Map(); variations.set(base, variants); }
42	        variants.set(key.slice(dollar + 1), value);
43	        continue;
44	      }
45	      texts.set(fullKey, value);
46	      list.push(fullKey);
47	    }
48	    groupedKeys.set(cat, list);
49	  }
50	  return { texts, groupedKeys, variations };
51	}
52	
53	class LanguageManager {
54	  activeCulture: LangCultureInfo | null = null;
55	  cultures: LangCultureInfo[] = [];            // 来自 public/l10n/index.json(设置面板数据源)
56	  private pack: LoadedPack = { texts: new Map(), groupedKeys: new Map(), variations: new Map() };
57	  private packCache = new Map<string, LoadedPack>(); // name → LoadedPack,Map 序即 LRU 序
58	  private listeners: Array<() => void> = [];
59	  private warned = new Set<string>();
60	  loaded = false;
61	
62	  /**
63	   * 启动初始化:加载语言列表 + 当前语言包。
64	   * lang 未指定时用 DEFAULT_LANG(zh-Hans);非法值归一到 en-US。
65	   */
66	  async init(lang?: string | number): Promise<boolean> {
67	    await this.loadIndex();
68	    return this.setLanguage(lang ?? 'zh-Hans');
69	  }
70	
71	  private async loadIndex() {
72	    if (this.cultures.length) return;
73	    try {
74	      const r = await fetch('l10n/index.json');
75	      if (r.ok) this.cultures = await r.json();
76	    } catch { /* 列表缺失时仍可用 GameCulture 静态表 */
77	      if (!this.cultures.length) {
78	        const { CULTURES } = await import('./GameCulture');
79	        this.cultures = CULTURES;
80	      }
81	    }
82	  }
83	
84	  /** 切换语言(culture 对象 | culture 名 | legacyId;LanguageManager.cs:54/60/92) */
85	  async setLanguage(culture: LangCultureInfo | string | number): Promise<boolean> {
86	    const name = typeof culture === 'object' ? culture.name : normalizeLang(culture);
87	    if (this.activeCulture?.name === name && this.loaded) return true;
88	    const tables = await this.fetchPack(name);
89	    if (!tables) return false;
90	    this.pack = tables;
91	    this.activeCulture = this.cultures.find((c) => c.name === name) ?? { name, legacyId: 0, displayNameKey: '' };
92	    this.loaded = true;
93	    this.warned.clear();
94	    this.emit();
95	    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.dispatchEvent(new Event('sw:langchange'));
96	    return true;
97	  }
98	
99	  private async fetchPack(name: string): Promise<LoadedPack | null> {
100	    const cached = this.packCache.get(name);
101	    if (cached) {
102	      this.packCache.delete(name);
103	      this.packCache.set(name, cached); // 刷新 LRU 序
104	      return cached;
105	    }
106	    try {
107	      const r = await fetch(`l10n/${name}.json`);
108	      if (!r.ok) return null;
109	      const pack = (await r.json()) as Pack;
110	      const tables = buildTables(pack);
111	      this.packCache.set(name, tables);
112	      while (this.packCache.size > MAX_PACK_CACHE) {
113	        const oldest = this.packCache.keys().next().value as string;
114	        if (oldest === name) break;
115	        this.packCache.delete(oldest);
116	      }
117	      return tables;
118	    } catch {
119	      return null;
120	    }
121	  }
122	
123	  /** 测试注入入口(对标 LoadLanguageFromFileTextJson:可连续调用模拟原版顺序加载覆盖) */
124	  loadPackJson(pack: Pack) {
125	    for (const [cat, entries] of Object.entries(pack)) {
126	      const list = this.pack.groupedKeys.get(cat) ?? [];
127	      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(entries)) {
128	        const fullKey = `${cat}.${key}`;
129	        const dollar = key.indexOf('$');
130	        if (dollar > 0) {
131	          const base = `${cat}.${key.slice(0, dollar)}`;
132	          let variants = this.pack.variations.get(base);
133	          if (!variants) { variants = new Map(); this.pack.variations.set(base, variants); }
134	          variants.set(key.slice(dollar + 1), value);
135	          continue;
136	        }
137	        if (!this.pack.texts.has(fullKey)) list.push(fullKey);
138	        this.pack.texts.set(fullKey, value);
139	      }
140	      this.pack.groupedKeys.set(cat, list);
141	    }
142	  }
143	
144	  /** 重置为测试初始态(含 pack 缓存) */
145	  resetForTest() {
146	    this.pack = { texts: new Map(), groupedKeys: new Map(), variations: new Map() };
147	    this.activeCulture = null;
148	    this.loaded = false;
149	    this.warned.clear();
150	    this.packCache.clear();
151	  }
152	
153	  exists(key: string): boolean {
154	    return this.pack.texts.has(key);
155	  }
156	
157	  /** 取原文(不做 {0} 参数替换)。缺失返回 key 本身(LanguageManager.cs:446-453) */
158	  getText(key: string): string {
159	    const v = this.pack.texts.get(key);
160	    if (v === undefined) {
161	      if (!this.warned.has(key)) { this.warned.add(key); console.warn(`[Lang] 缺失 key: ${key}`); }
162	      return key;
163	    }
164	    return v;
165	  }
166	
167	  /** 取文案并做 {0}/{1} 位置参数替换(LanguageManager.cs:455-498 GetTextValue) */
168	  getTextValue(key: string, ...args: (string | number)[]): string {
169	    let s = this.getText(key);
170	    for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) s = s.split(`{${i}}`).join(String(args[i]));
171	    return s;
172	  }
173	
174	  /** $ 变体查询(对标 TryGetVariation,LanguageManager.cs:509) */
175	  tryGetVariation(baseKey: string, variant: string): string | null {
176	    return this.pack.variations.get(baseKey)?.get(variant) ?? null;
177	  }
178	
179	  /** 类别内取模循环取值(对标 IndexedFromCategory,LanguageManager.cs:431) */
180	  indexedFromCategory(category: string, index: number): string | null {
181	    const list = this.pack.groupedKeys.get(category);
182	    if (!list || !list.length) return null;
183	    const i = ((index % list.length) + list.length) % list.length;
184	    return this.pack.texts.get(list[i]) ?? null;
185	  }
186	
187	  /** 类别内随机(对标 RandomFromCategory,LanguageManager.cs:422) */
188	  randomFromCategory(category: string, rng: () => number = Math.random): string | null {
189	    const list = this.pack.groupedKeys.get(category);
190	    if (!list || !list.length) return null;
191	    return this.pack.texts.get(list[Math.floor(rng() * list.length)]) ?? null;
192	  }
193	
194	  /** Lang.CreateDialogFilter(startsWith)（Lang.cs:425-428）+ Language.SelectRandom：
195	   *  类目内全键前缀匹配后随机一条（NPC.HasSpecialEventText 的
196	   *  "XxxSpecialText.Yyy" 族——键形如 MerchantSpecialText.Rain1/Rain2） */
197	  randomFromPrefix(prefix: string, rng: () => number = Math.random): string | null {
198	    const dot = prefix.indexOf('.');
199	    if (dot <= 0) return null;
200	    const list = this.pack.groupedKeys.get(prefix.slice(0, dot));
201	    if (!list || !list.length) return null;
202	    const hits = list.filter((k) => k.startsWith(prefix));
203	    if (!hits.length) return null;
204	    return this.pack.texts.get(hits[Math.floor(rng() * hits.length)]) ?? null;
205	  }
206	
207	  categorySize(category: string): number {
208	    return this.pack.groupedKeys.get(category)?.length ?? 0;
209	  }
210	
211	  /** OnLanguageChanged(LanguageManager.cs:38)。返回退订函数 */
212	  onChange(fn: () => void): () => void {
213	    this.listeners.push(fn);
214	    return () => { this.listeners = this.listeners.filter((f) => f !== fn); };
215	  }
216	
217	  private emit() {
218	    for (const fn of this.listeners) fn();
219	  }
220	}
221	
222	export const languageManager = new LanguageManager();
223	export { DEFAULT_CULTURE_NAME };
224	export type { GameCulture };
225

34	  addLight(x: number, y: number, r: number, g: number, b: number): void {
35	    this.perFrame.push({ x, y, r, g, b });
36	    this.dirty = true;

38	
39	  /** 本帧已注册的动态点光数（F5 调试报告只读 perFrame 长度） */
40	  get perFrameCount(): number { return this.perFrame.length; }
41	
42	  // ---- 内部 ----
43	  private state: State = State.MinimapUpdate;
44	  private work = new LightMap();
45	  private active = new LightMap();
46	  private flicker = new FlickerClock();
47	
48	  /** 全局闪烁时钟(mouseTextColor/cursorScale 等),资源条 HUD 等渲染层读取 */
49	  get clock(): FlickerClock { return this.flicker; }
50	  private scanner: TileLightScanner;

52	  private liqTick = 0;
53	
54	  constructor(world: LightingWorld) {
55	    this.world = world;
56	    this.scanner = new TileLightScanner(this.flicker);
57	    world.store.onTileChanged(() => { this.dirty = true; });
58	    world.store.onLiquidChanged(() => { this.liquidDirty = true; });
59	  }
60	
61	  /**
62	   * 区域 = 相机视口 ± AREA_PADDING(原版 GetAreaToLight 边界 1..2 + ProcessScan 膨胀 28 合并)。
63	   * camCX/camCY 为像素;viewW/viewH 为 tile 数(Game 现有调用约定)。
64	   */
65	  ensureRegion(camCX: number, camCY: number, viewW: number, viewH: number): void {
66	    const st = this.world.store;
67	    const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(camCX / TILE) - Math.ceil(viewW / 2) - 1 - AREA_PADDING);
68	    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(camCY / TILE) - Math.ceil(viewH / 2) - 1 - AREA_PADDING);
69	    const x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor(camCX / TILE) + Math.ceil(viewW / 2) + 2 + AREA_PADDING);
70	    const y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor(camCY / TILE) + Math.ceil(viewH / 2) + 2 + AREA_PADDING);
71	    const rw = x1 - x0 + 1, rh = y1 - y0 + 1;
72	    if (rw !== this.rw || rh !== this.rh || x0 !== this.rx || y0 !== this.ry) {
73	      // 原点只在重算时原子移动(防光影相对世界平移)
74	      this.rx = x0; this.ry = y0;
75	      if (rw !== this.rw || rh !== this.rh) {
76	        this.rw = rw; this.rh = rh;
77	        this.r = new Uint8Array(rw * rh);
78	        this.g = new Uint8Array(rw * rh);
79	        this.b = new Uint8Array(rw * rh);
80	      }
81	      this.dirty = true;
82	    }
83	  }
84	
85	  /** 每帧调用。dayFactor 参数弃用(天空色改由 clock 内部推导;旧调用方签名兼容)。 */
86	  compute(_dayFactor: number): void {
87	    if (this.rw === 0) return;
88	    this.flicker.tick();
89	
90	    if (this.dirty) {
91	      this.runFullCycle();
92	      return;
93	    }
94	    if (this.liquidDirty) {
95	      this.liqTick++;
96	      if (this.liqTick % 3 !== 0) return; // 20Hz 节流
97	      this.runFullCycle();
98	      return;
99	    }
100	    // 空闲:每 tick 一态(完整周期 4 tick,原版 4 帧节奏)
101	    this.step();
102	  }
103	
104	  /** 快进:同 tick 完成 Scan→Blur→Present(状态回 0) */
105	  private runFullCycle(): void {
106	    this.dirty = false;
107	    this.liquidDirty = false;
108	    this.liqTick = 0;
109	    this.processScan();
110	    this.processBlurAndPresent();
111	    this.state = State.MinimapUpdate;
112	  }
113	
114	  private step(): void {
115	    switch (this.state) {
116	      case State.MinimapUpdate: // 空槽(小地图导出未移植;保留维持 4 tick 周期)
117	      case State.ExportMetrics: // 空槽(SceneMetrics 在 VanillaSpawner 独立实现)
118	        break;
119	      case State.Scan:
120	        this.processScan();
121	        break;
122	      case State.Blur:
123	        this.processBlurAndPresent();
124	        break;
125	    }
126	    this.state = (this.state + 1) % 4;
127	  }
128	
129	  private processScan(): void {
130	    const w = this.world as ScannerWorld;
131	    this.work.setSize(this.rw, this.rh);
132	    this.work.nonVisiblePadding = Math.min(NON_VISIBLE_PADDING, (Math.min(this.rw, this.rh) - 1) >> 1);
133	    // 手持光源:RGB 来自 TorchID.TorchColor(原版 ItemCheck_EmitHeldItemLight 按火把种类取色)
134	    const perFrame = this.perFrame;
135	    if (this.heldLight) perFrame.push({ x: this.heldLight.tx, y: this.heldLight.ty, r: this.heldLight.r, g: this.heldLight.g, b: this.heldLight.b });
136	    this.scanner.exportTo(w, this.work, this.rx, this.ry, perFrame);
137	    perFrame.length = 0;
138	  }
139	
140	  private processBlurAndPresent(): void {
141	    this.work.blur();
142	    // Present(LightingEngine.cs:131-135):交换双缓冲 + 导出 ×1.2 到公共面
143	    const tmp = this.active;
144	    this.active = this.work;
145	    this.work = tmp;
146	    const outR = this.r, outG = this.g, outB = this.b;
147	    const ar = this.active.r, ag = this.active.g, ab = this.active.b;
148	    const scale = GLOBAL_BRIGHTNESS * 255;
149	    for (let i = 0; i < outR.length; i++) {
150	      outR[i] = Math.min(255, Math.round(ar[i] * scale));
151	      outG[i] = Math.min(255, Math.round(ag[i] * scale));
152	      outB[i] = Math.min(255, Math.round(ab[i] * scale));
153	    }
154	  }
155	
156	  /** 取某 tile 光照(区域外返回 0;原版 GetColor 区域外语义) */
157	  lightAt(tx: number, ty: number, _dayFactor = 0): [number, number, number] {
158	    if (tx < this.rx || ty < this.ry || tx >= this.rx + this.rw || ty >= this.ry + this.rh) return [0, 0, 0];
159	    const i = (ty - this.ry) * this.rw + (tx - this.rx);
160	    return [this.r[i], this.g[i], this.b[i]];
161	  }
162	}

  draw(r: Renderer, world: World) {

        }
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    void world;
  }

}

function saveGameCompat(g: Game): string {

637	    const mapPaintPacked = (packed: number, colorType: number, isWall: boolean): number => {

708	  flushDirty() {
709	    if (this.dirtyChunks.size === 0) return;
710	    const st = this.world.store;
711	    let n = 0;
712	    for (const k of this.dirtyChunks) {
713	      if (n++ > 24) break;
714	      this.dirtyChunks.delete(k);
715	      const cx = (k & 0xffff) << 16 >> 16, cy = (k >> 16) << 16 >> 16;
716	      const x0 = cx * CHUNK, y0 = cy * CHUNK;
717	      for (let y = y0; y < y0 + CHUNK; y++) {
718	        for (let x = x0; x < x0 + CHUNK; x++) {
719	          if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
720	          const c = this.colorFor(x, y);
721	          this.ctx.fillStyle = c ?? '#000';
722	          this.ctx.fillRect(x, y, 1, 1);
723	        }
724	      }
725	    }
726	  }
727	}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T05:55:02.490Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Minimap:
- redrawAll is 50ms-ish (known). 
- `flushDirty` — 24 chunks per frame max, each chunk = 256 fillRect calls (16×16)! That's 24×256 = 6144 `fillStyle`+`fillRect` per frame worst case. Each colorFor returns a string (allocations). During liquid flow / mining, dirtyChunks grows; consumption capped at 24/frame. If marking outpaces, dirtyChunks Set grows — bounded by chunk count in world (~19650 for large world). Not infinite but can grow large; consumption is slow. More important: **fillRect per pixel** is slow vs putImageData. Moderate.

- Minimap full-canvas = world.w × world.h canvas: 6400×1800 = 11.5M pixels × 4 = 46MB. Plus createImageData in redrawAll = another 46MB transient. Plus a new `Uint32Array` view (no copy). OK.

Now let me check drawFullMap to see the cost when opening the full map.
```

</details>

5119	        fm.anchorU = (mouseX - (viewW / 2 - (this._fmWorldW * fm.zoom) / 2 + fm.panX)) / fm.zoom;
5120	        fm.anchorV = (mouseY - (viewH / 2 - (this._fmWorldH * fm.zoom) / 2 + fm.panY)) / fm.zoom;

```
  private drawFog(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, world: World, ox: number, oy: number, scale: number, blockPx: number, clip?: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) {
    const fc = this.getFogCanvas(world);
    if (!fc) return;
    ctx.save();
    if (clip) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.rect(clip.x, clip.y, clip.w, clip.h);
      ctx.clip();
    }
    void blockPx;
    // 缓存画布 1 像素 = 2 tile；目标绘制按 scale×2 缩放
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    ctx.drawImage(fc, 0, 0, fc.width, fc.height, ox, oy, fc.width * 2 * scale, fc.height * 2 * scale);
    ctx.restore();
  }

```

182	      const tk = this.game.input.touchKeys;
183	      tk.delete('KeyA'); tk.delete('KeyD'); tk.delete('KeyW'); tk.delete('KeyS');
184	      if (this.axis.x > 0.35) tk.add('KeyD');
185	      else if (this.axis.x < -0.35) tk.add('KeyA');
186	      if (this.axis.y < -0.55) tk.add('KeyW');
187	      else if (this.axis.y > 0.55) tk.add('KeyS');
188	    };
189	    const move = (t: Touch) => {
190	      const r = joy.getBoundingClientRect();
191	      let dx = (t.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2)) / R;
192	      let dy = (t.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2)) / R;
193	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
194	      if (d > 1) { dx /= d; dy /= d; }
195	      this.axis.x = dx; this.axis.y = dy;
196	      knob.style.transform = `translate(calc(-50% + ${dx * R}px), calc(-50% + ${dy * R}px))`;
197	      apply();
198	    };
199	    joy.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
200	      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
201	      const t = e.changedTouches[0];
202	      this.joyId = t.identifier;
203	      move(t);
204	    }, { passive: false });
205	    joy.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => {
206	      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
207	      for (const t of Array.from(e.changedTouches)) {
208	        if (t.identifier === this.joyId) move(t);
209	      }
210	    }, { passive: false });
211	    const end = (e: TouchEvent) => {
212	      for (const t of Array.from(e.changedTouches)) {
213	        if (t.identifier !== this.joyId) continue;
214	        e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
215	        this.joyId = null;
216	        this.axis.x = 0; this.axis.y = 0;
217	        knob.style.transform = 'translate(-50%,-50%)';
218	        apply();
219	      }
220	    };
221	    joy.addEventListener('touchend', end, { passive: false });
222	    joy.addEventListener('touchcancel', end, { passive: false });
223	    this.root.appendChild(joy);
224	  }
225

228	    b.className = 'sw-mjump';
229	    b.textContent = '⤒';
230	    this.bindButton(b, () => this.game.input.touchKeys.add('Space'), () => this.game.input.touchKeys.delete('Space'));
231	    this.root.appendChild(b);

313	  /** 按钮态刷新：药水可用亮起 / 智能光标激活 / 钩爪·坐骑按装备显隐 */
314	  private refresh(): void {
315	    const g = this.game;
316	    // 药水：有治疗药水 → 显示；受伤时亮起
317	    let hasHeal = false;
318	    for (const s of g.player.inv.slots) {
319	      if (!s) continue;
320	      const ff = itemFuncOfVid(vanillaIdOfItem(s.id));
321	      if (ff?.consumable && ff.healLife && !ff.createTile && !ff.shoot && !ff.pick && !ff.axe && !ff.hammer) {
322	        hasHeal = true;
323	        break;
324	      }
325	    }
326	    if (this.potionBtn) {
327	      this.potionBtn.style.display = hasHeal ? '' : 'none';
328	      this.potionBtn.classList.toggle('lit', hasHeal && g.player.hp < g.player.maxHp);
329	      this.potionBtn.classList.toggle('off', !hasHeal || g.player.buffs.healBlocked);
330	    }
331	    if (this.smartBtn) this.smartBtn.classList.toggle('on', !!g.smartCursor.wanted);
332	    if (this.grapBtn) this.grapBtn.style.display = g.player.inv.miscEquips[4] ? '' : 'none';
333	    if (this.rideBtn) {
334	      const ride = g.player.inv.miscEquips[3] || g.player.inv.miscEquips[0];
335	      this.rideBtn.style.display = ride ? '' : 'none';
336	    }
337	  }

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T05:55:37.869Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
MobileControls: 
- Joystick `move` handler: `joy.getBoundingClientRect()` called on EVERY touchmove — forces layout read (potential layout thrash if combined with the subsequent style write `knob.style.transform`). On mobile, touchmove fires at input rate (60-120Hz). getBoundingClientRect + style write per event = potential forced synchronous layout each time. Since knob transform changes layout? transform doesn't trigger layout, but the read could still be a reflow if anything was invalidated. Moderate on mobile — a known pattern for jank. Also `transition:transform 90ms ease` on the knob while being moved per-event → transition restart churn (style recalc + composite animation restarts). Cheap fix: cache rect on touchstart; disable transition during drag.
- refresh() every 1s — iterates inventory (~50 slots) and itemFuncOfVid — fine.

Now ClientNet. Let me read it (or key parts): msg broadcast frequency, AOI, puppets.
```

</details>

140	  /** 底层建连（首连/重连共用） */
141	  private connect0(url: string) {

144	    try {
145	      ws = new WebSocket(url);
146	    } catch (e) {
147	      // R12 修复：非法 URL 同步抛异常 → 转为 reject 语义（onKick）而非穿透调用栈

152	    ws.binaryType = 'arraybuffer';

155	    if (!this.pingTimer) {

166	      // （非房主/旧路径传空串，服务器 try/catch 读不到即跳过）
167	      const hello = new NetWriter(Msg.Hello).str(PROTO_MAGIC).u16(PROTO_VER).str(name).str(this.hostToken);
168	      this.send(hello.finish());
169	    };
170	    ws.onmessage = (e) => {
171	      this.parser.append(new Uint8Array(e.data as ArrayBuffer));
172	      this.parser.drain((id, r) => this.handle(id, r));
173	    };
174	    ws.onclose = () => {
175	      if (!this.active || this.userClosed) return;
176	      // v3 断线重连（§8.8）：有 session 凭据 → 2s 间隔自动重试 3 次（同 slot 复位）；
177	      // 耗尽或无凭据 → 按原语义 onKick
178	      if (this.session && this.reconnects < 3) {
179	        this.reconnects++;
180	        this.active = false;
181	        setTimeout(() => {
182	          if (this.userClosed) return;
183	          const sep = this.url.includes('?') ? '&' : '?';
184	          this.connect0(`${this.url}${sep}session=${encodeURIComponent(this.session)}`);
185	        }, 2000);
186	        return;
187	      }
188	      this.active = false;
189	      this.hooks.onKick?.('与服务器断开连接');
190	    };
191	    ws.onerror = () => { /* close 跟上 */ };
192	  }

204	  requestSection(cx: number, cy: number) {

206	  }

216	  // ================= 收包分发（对齐原版 MessageBuffer switch） =================
217	
218	  private handle(msgId: number, r: NetReader) {
219	    switch (msgId) {
220	      case Msg.Kick: {
221	        this.hooks.onKick?.(r.str());
222	        this.disconnect();
223	        return;
224	      }
225	      case Msg.PlayerSlot: {
226	        this.mySlot = r.u8();
227	        this.invDumpPending = true; // 物品全量 dump 时机：slot 分配后首 tick
228	        // v3：session 凭据（重连用）；重连场景复位 join 状态以重走世界组装
229	        const sess = (() => { try { return r.str(); } catch { return ''; } })();
230	        if (sess) {
231	          if (this.worldDelivered) {
232	            // 断线重连：世界将重新组装下发（pendingWorld 复位）
233	            this.worldDelivered = false;
234	            this.pendingWorld = null;
235	            this.pendingStrips = 0;
236	          }
237	          this.session = sess;
238	          this.reconnects = 0;
239	        }
240	        // 全量上传自身（对齐原版 msg3 后立刻 SyncPlayer + RequestWorldData）
241	        const p2 = this.game.player as { appearance?: unknown } | undefined | null;
242	        const app = JSON.stringify(p2?.appearance ?? {});
243	        this.send(new NetWriter(Msg.SyncPlayer).u8(this.mySlot).str(app).finish());
244	        this.send(new NetWriter(Msg.RequestWorldData).finish());
245	        return;
246	      }
247	      case Msg.WorldData: {
248	        this.pendingWorld = this.readWorldData(r);
249	        return;
250	      }
251	      case Msg.StatusText: {
252	        this.pendingStrips = r.u16();
253	        this.pendingStripsTotal = Math.max(1, this.pendingStrips);
254	        // 仅初始组装期上报进度：进世界后 R3 移动续传也会触发 StatusText（fresh=0），
255	        // 无条件回调会把已关闭的全屏进度遮罩重新拉起 → "永久卡在接收世界数据"
256	        if (!this.worldDelivered) this.hooks.onProgress?.('接收世界数据', 0);

**📎 ToolResult**

```
 *  一轮、按列切片轮转（每轮预算 cellsPerRound 格，K 轮覆盖全图），每苗每轮命中概率
 *  gemP/sapP = K×300×rate×(w×h)/区格数（×590 命中再掷 1/5、20 苗命中掷 1/20），
 *  单 tick 期望 = gemP/(K×300) 与原版逐点相等——数值等价实现（等价推导登记于
 *  growSaplings 函数头），空间上不再限域玩家附近 */
export function saplingScanPlan(wTiles: number, hTiles: number, groundLevel: number,
  round: number, cellsPerRound = 120000, raining = false): {
  K: number; sliceW: number; x0: number; x1: number;
  underY0: number; underY1: number; overY0: number; overY1: number;
  gemP: number; sapP: number;
} {
  // 采样区格数（原版 y 取值域宽度 × w）：地下 j∈[worldSurface-1, h-20)，
  // 地表 j∈[10, worldSurface-1)（WorldGen.cs:71553-71555/:71627-71629）
  const underTiles = Math.max(1, (hTiles - Math.floor(groundLevel) - 19) * wTiles);
  const overTiles = Math.max(1, (Math.floor(groundLevel) - 11) * wTiles);
  // 每列需扫行数（地表带+地下带）→ 每轮列数预算 → K 轮覆盖全图
  const rows = Math.max(1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(groundLevel) - 11)
    + Math.max(0, hTiles - Math.floor(groundLevel) - 19));
  const sliceW = Math.max(1, Math.floor(cellsPerRound / rows));
  const K = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(wTiles / sliceW));
  const slice = round % K;
  const x0 = Math.max(0, slice * sliceW);
  const x1 = Math.min(wTiles, x0 + sliceW);
  return {
    K, sliceW, x0, x1,
    underY0: Math.floor(groundLevel) - 1, underY1: hTiles - 20,   // [gl-1, h-21]
    overY0: 10, overY1: Math.floor(groundLevel) - 1,              // [10, gl-2]
    // 每苗每轮命中概率（K 轮一访 × 300 tick/轮 × 原版单 tick 采样率 × 命中掷骰）
    gemP: Math.min(1, K * 300 * 1.5e-05 * (wTiles * hTiles) / underTiles * 0.2),
    sapP: Math.min(1, K * 300 * 3e-05 * (wTiles * hTiles) / overTiles * (1 / 20)
      * (raining ? 1.5 : 1)),
  };
}

const TELEPORT_ITEM_VIDS = new Set([50, 3124, 3199, 5358, 2350, 2351, 4263, 4819, 5359, 5360, 5361]);

function dropVanillaId(d: ItemDrop): number {
  return slotVanillaId(d.itemId);
}

/** 内部 item id → 原版 item id（vi_<id>_ 键解析;非 vi_ 键恒 -1） */
function slotVanillaId(itemId: number): number {
  return viIdFromKey(ITEM_DEFS[itemId]?.key ?? '');
}

/** 配饰槽（armor[3..9]，原版 ApplyEquipFunctional :12543-12550）→ 原版 item id 列表 */
export function accessoryVanillaIds(p: Player): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let acc = ACC_ARMOR_START; acc < p.inv.accSlotMax; acc++) {
    const s = p.inv.armor[acc];
    if (s) out.push(slotVanillaId(s.id));
  }
  return out;
}

/** 星光斗篷族反击态（Player.cs:37826-37863）：active = 装备任一族件
 *  （starCloak 880 / starVeil 929 / beeCloak 1247 / manaCloak 982——accfx 仅 880/1247
 *  带 starCloak 位，929/982 会漏检，故并入装备 id 扫描）；
 *  projType 按 override 件优先级 manaCloak 723 > beeCloak 724 > starVeil 725 > 726 */
export function starCloakStarInfo(accVids: number[], starCloakFlag: boolean): { active: boolean; projType: number } {
  const veil = accVids.includes(929), bee = accVids.includes(1247), mana = accVids.includes(982);
  return {
    active: starCloakFlag || veil || bee || mana,
    projType: mana ? 723 : bee ? 724 : veil ? 725 : 726,
  };
}

/** 蜂巢族受击反击掷骰（Player.cs:37871-37907）：数量 num19 =
 *  1 + (Next(3)==0) + (Next(3)==0) + (strongBees(HivePack 3333) && Next(3)==0)；
 *  基伤 num20 = strongBees ? 18 : 13（专家 ×1.5/大师 ×2 由调用侧难度档处理） */
export function beeRetaliateRoll(strongBees: boolean, rnd: () => number): { count: number; base: number } {
  let count = 1;
  if (Math.floor(rnd() * 3) === 0) count++;
  if (Math.floor(rnd() * 3) === 0) count++;
  if (strongBees && Math.floor(rnd() * 3) === 0) count++;
  return { count, base: strongBees ? 18 : 13 };
}

/** 幸运币近战命中掉币掷骰（Player.cs:43892-43916）：币种 71 铜 / Next(10)==0 升 72 银 /
 *  Next(100)==0 升 73 金（顺序覆盖）；stack = Next(1,11)（1-10）；
 *  velocity.Y = Next(-20,1)*0.2、X = Next(10,31)*0.2*direction（玩家朝向）。
 *  门（value>0 && hasLuckyCoin && Next(5)==0）由调用侧判定 */
export function luckyCoinRoll(rnd: () => number, direction: number): { key: string; stack: number; vx: number; vy: number } {
  let key = VI_KEY.COIN.COPPER;
  if (Math.floor(rnd() * 10) === 0) key = VI_KEY.COIN.SILVER;
  if (Math.floor(rnd() * 100) === 0) key = VI_KEY.COIN.GOLD;
  return {
    key,
    stack: 1 + Math.floor(rnd() * 10),
    vx: (10 + Math.floor(rnd() * 21)) * 0.2 * direction,
    vy: (Math.floor(rnd() * 21) - 20) * 0.2,
  };
}

/** 玩家储物族 tile key → bank 索引（原版 Player.cs:32598+：29→bank / 97→bank2 /
 *  463→bank3 / 491→bank4，chest 槽位约定 -2..-5） */
const BANK_TILES: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, number]> = [
  ['v_29_piggy_bank', 0], ['v_97_safe', 1], ['v_463_defenders_forge', 2], ['v_491_void_vault', 3],
];

/** 就地剔除 life<=0 的粒子/飘字(保序零分配,2026-08 审计 G9) */
function compactByLife<T extends { life: number }>(list: T[]): void {
  let w = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < list.length; r++) {
    if (list[r].life > 0) list[w++] = list[r];
  }
  list.length = w;
}

/** 中核/硬核死亡掉落全部物品（Player.DropItems :53398-53470 gemsOnly=false 分支）：
 *  背包+盔甲+染料+杂项装备+杂项染料+三套 Loadout 全部以掉落物形式撒在死亡点
 *  （TryDroppingSingleItem :53480-53496：随机抛射速度由调用方注入），随后发还
 *  铜制起始三件套（:53463-53468 inventory[0]=3507 短剑/[1]=3509 镐/[2]=3506 斧）。
 *  软核/旅程走 gemsOnly=true 只掉宝石（1522-1527/3643）——本仓无背包宝石，近似为不掉。
 *  spawn 回调注入使其可脱离 Game 实例单测（key+stack 逐件回调） */
export function dropInventoryOnDeath(
  inv: import('../items/Inventory').Inventory,
  spawn: (key: string, stack: number) => void,
): void {
  const drop = (slots: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>) => {
    for (const s of slots) {
      if (!s || s.id === 0 || s.stack <= 0) continue;
      // 槽位存的是内部 id → 先查内部表（自定义物品 key 唯一权威）；
      // VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID 以 vanilla id 为键，不能反过来用（内部 id 撞号会掉错物品）
      const key = ITEM_DEFS[s.id]?.key ?? VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[s.id];
      if (key) spawn(key, s.stack);
    }
  };
  drop(inv.slots);
  drop(inv.armor);
  drop(inv.dye);
  drop(inv.miscEquips);
  drop(inv.miscDyes);
  for (const l of inv.loadouts) { drop(l.armor); drop(l.dye); }  // EquipmentLoadout.TryDroppingItems
  inv.slots.fill(null);
  inv.armor.fill(null);
  inv.dye.fill(null);

```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T05:56:15.306Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
HitTile: CAP=500 LRU, TTL prune. BUT: `update()` does `for (const [key, e] of [...this.data])` — allocates a full array copy of up to 500 entries EVERY update (every 2 ticks, line 2150 `if (this.tickCount % 2 === 0) this.hitTiles.update()`). That's a 500-element array + 500 tuple arrays + iterator allocation every 2 ticks = 30 times/sec. GC churn: 15,000 tuple-arrays/sec. This is a genuine per-frame allocation hotspot (G9-class that was missed). Severity: 中. Cheap fix: iterate with early-snapshot of keys only (`[...this.data.keys()]`) — still allocates but 1 array of strings instead of entries; better: keep a parallel array or iterate keys() and delete during iteration is safe in JS Maps (deleting current entry during iteration is allowed). Actually deleting during for...of on a Map IS safe. So `[...this.data]` copy is unnecessary entirely.

Also addDamage allocates a string key `${x},${y},${type}` per call — mining hot path. Fine-ish.

World.store: new TileStore per world load (line 272/287) → listeners don't accumulate across loads. 误报排除 for TileStore.listeners.

BUT: ChunkCache constructor registers `world.store.onTileChanged(...)` — per world. And Game.ts:1784/1792/7895 register on `w.store` inside loadWorld (called once per world load). Need to verify 1784/1792/7895 are inside loadWorld, not somewhere repeated. Line 1617 is loadWorld; 1784 is inside. OK.

// 实体基类与分桶管理器
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';
import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';

export abstract class Entity {
  id = 0;
  x = 0; y = 0;          // 左上角 px
  w = 16; h = 16;
  vx = 0; vy = 0;
  dead = false;
  onGround = false;
  hitWall = false;
  hitHead = false;
  dropThrough = false;
  /** 液体位移减速因子（原版 waterMovementSpeed 族，NPC.cs:5946 默认 水/岩浆 .5、
   *  蜂蜜 .25、微光 .375）。1=dry 不减速；Enemy 侧 npcLiquid.updateNpcLiquid 每 tick 写，
   *  moveAndCollide 入口缩 dx/dy（velocity 不变——同 Collision_MoveWhileWet :93986） */
  liqFactor = 1;
  // ---- 联机傀儡标记（v3 实体同步）：快照驱动实体，本地不跑 AI/命中判定 ----
  netPuppet = false;
  /** 联机实体号（拥有者 slot 高 8 位 + 本地 id 低 24 位；见 net/entitySyncHooks） */
  netId = 0;
  // ---- P3.2 傀儡插值缓冲（15Hz 快照 × 60TPS 渲染的平滑桥）----
  /** 最新权威快照位（NPC 接触判定用；弹幕侧判定用渲染位——见 Game 两处接触注释） */
  netAx = 0; netAy = 0;
  /** 插值起点（快照到达时的渲染位）与进度（1=已到目标，纯外推） */
  netIx = 0; netIy = 0; netIt = 1;
  private netSnapIvt = 4;

  abstract fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void;
  abstract draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void;

  /** 快照到达（Game.netApplyNpcSnaps / netApplyProjSnaps 调用）：设插值目标。
   *  从当前渲染位连续出发（中途到包不跳变）；intervalTicks = 快照周期（15Hz=4 tick） */
  netSnapTo(x: number, y: number, intervalTicks = 4) {
    this.netIx = this.x; this.netIy = this.y;
    this.netAx = x; this.netAy = y;
    this.netIt = 0;
    this.netSnapIvt = Math.max(1, intervalTicks);
  }

  /** 傀儡每 tick 推进：未到目标走 smoothstep 插值（一个快照周期到达），到后纯速度外推 */
  netPuppetStep() {
    if (this.netIt < 1) {
      this.netIt = Math.min(1, this.netIt + 1 / this.netSnapIvt);
      const t = this.netIt * this.netIt * (3 - 2 * this.netIt); // smoothstep
      this.x = this.netIx + (this.netAx - this.netIx) * t;
      this.y = this.netIy + (this.netAy - this.netIy) * t;
      return;
    }
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
  }

  get cx() { return this.x + this.w / 2; }
  get cy() { return this.y + this.h / 2; }
  aabbOverlaps(o: Entity): boolean {
    return this.x < o.x + o.w && this.x + this.w > o.x && this.y < o.y + o.h && this.y + this.h > o.y;
  }
}

/** 就地剔除 dead 实体(保持顺序,零分配) */
function compact(list: Entity[]): void {
  let w = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < list.length; r++) {
    const e = list[r];
    if (!e.dead) list[w++] = e;
  }
  list.length = w;
}

export class EntityManager {
  nextId = 1;
  enemies: Entity[] = [];
  drops: Entity[] = [];
  npcs: Entity[] = [];
  projectiles: Entity[] = [];
  props: Entity[] = [];   // 场景物件（墓碑等不可拾取物理实体）
  critters: Entity[] = []; // 小动物（无害被动生物）

  /** 精灵底部透明留白行数（缓存）：用于脚底对齐 */
  private bottomPadCache = new Map<string, number>();

  /** 逐像素染色缓存（原版 XNA SpriteBatch 的 color 乘法等价实现）：
   *  ① multiply 把 RGB 乘上 color —— canvas multiply 会把精灵透明区也铺色（方形伪影）
   *  ② destination-in 重画精灵本体 → alpha 恢复为精灵轮廓，裁掉①的方形
   *  alpha 保持精灵原样（XNA 预乘混合的 RGB 贡献不随 color.A 缩减，见 drawEnemy color pass）
   *  （GetColor 二次绘制语义，Main.cs:24986 / NPC.cs:94903） */
  private tintCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();
  private tintedSprite(
    key: string, frame: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number }, color: number[],
  ): HTMLCanvasElement {
    const k = `${key}|${frame.sx},${frame.sy},${frame.sw},${frame.sh}|${color.join(',')}`;
    let c = this.tintCache.get(k);
    if (c) return c;
    c = document.createElement('canvas');
    c.width = Math.max(1, frame.sw);
    c.height = Math.max(1, frame.sh);
    const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
    cx.drawImage(frame.img, frame.sx, frame.sy, frame.sw, frame.sh, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    // ① RGB 乘 color（multiply 的 alpha 取并集 → 透明区被铺色，下一步裁掉）
    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
    cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${color[0]},${color[1]},${color[2]})`;
    cx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    // ② 用精灵本体当 destination-in 的源：alpha 恢复为轮廓形状
    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
    cx.drawImage(frame.img, frame.sx, frame.sy, frame.sw, frame.sh, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    if (this.tintCache.size > 1024) this.tintCache.clear();
    this.tintCache.set(k, c);
    return c;
  }

  // ================= 固定步逻辑 =================

  private fixedUpdate(dt: number) {
    this.tickCount++;
    // 对话距离保持:离开 NPC 超过 6 格自动关闭(原版 SetTalkNPC(-1) 语义)
    if (this.dialogNpc) {
      const dn = this.dialogNpc;
      if (dn.dead || Math.hypot(dn.cx - this.player.cx, dn.cy - this.player.cy) > TILE * 6) {
        this.closeNpcDialog();
      }
    }
    // 原版 HitTile 衰减（每 2 tick 一次——原版按帧衰减配原版 ~27 帧挥速；
    // 我们的挥击间隔更长，等比减半保持"连击可积累、停手会消退"）
    if (this.tickCount % 2 === 0) this.hitTiles.update();
    const w = this.world;
    // 下落沙:消费候选队列(有界,防止沙漠整柱同帧转换尖峰)
    if (this.sandQueue.length) this.drainSandQueue();
    // 时间推进倍率（Main.cs:6270-6294 UpdateTimeRate 1:1，经 World.dayRate()）：
    //   快进中（日晷/月晷 IsFastForwardingTime）恒 60；
    //   否则 FreezeTime 0 / TargetTimeRate × 全员熟睡 5（:6280-6285 SleepingPlayersCount
    //   ==ActivePlayersCount && >0——单人 = 本地玩家 FullyFallenAsleep）。
    // 熟睡计时（PlayerSleepingHelper.UpdateState :93-98：睡中 timeSleeping++；被打断归零。
    // DoesPlayerHaveReasonToActUpInBed :44-59 的危险/血月夜/日食昼门在此近似重置）
    if (this.player.sleeping) {
      const actUp = (w.clock.bloodMoon && !w.clock.isDay) || (w.clock.eclipse && w.clock.isDay)
        || w.lunarEvent.moonLordCountdown > 0 || w.invasionType > 0 || moonEventActive(w)
        || w.oldOnesArmy.ongoing || this.player.useTime > 0;
      this.player.timeSleeping = actUp ? 0 : this.player.timeSleeping + 1;
    } else {
      this.player.timeSleeping = 0;
    }
    // FullyFallenAsleep（PlayerSleepingHelper.cs:33-37：isSleeping && timeSleeping>=120）
    w.allSleeping = this.player.sleeping && this.player.timeSleeping >= 120;
    w.clock.tick(dt * 1000 * w.dayRate());
    // ---- 天气系统推进（原版 UpdateWeather/UpdateTime 每帧；60Hz tick 语义） ----
    this.updateWeather();
    // 昼夜切换：公告走原版消息列（Main.NewText→LegacyChatMonitor；ChatColors.World 50,255,130）
    const isDay = w.clock.isDay;
    if (isDay !== this.lastWasDay) {

  /** Game 每帧注入（weather 状态 + hooks） */
  attach(weather: WeatherState, hooks: WeatherFxHooks) {
    this.weather = weather;
    this.hooks = hooks;
  }
  detach() { this.weather = null; this.hooks = null; this.pool.length = 0; }

  /** 池容量 = 屏宽比例（原版 Main.maxRain 语义近似：屏宽/视口宽×25） */
  private ensurePool(cap: number) {
    this.poolCap = cap;
    while (this.pool.length < cap) {
      this.pool.push({ x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, scale: 1, rotation: 0, type: 0, active: false });
    }
  }

  /** Rain.MakeRain（Rain.cs:21-77）：每帧按 cloudAlpha 掷新滴 */
  private makeRain(viewW: number, camX: number, camY: number, playerVx: number, playerVy: number, world: World) {
    const w = this.weather;
    const hooks = this.hooks;
    if (!w || !hooks || w.cloudAlpha <= 0 || hooks.atmo < 0.4) return;
    let num = (viewW / 1920) * 25;               // MaxWorldViewSize.X≈1920 的比例
    num *= 0.25 + 1 * w.cloudAlpha;              // Rain.cs:28
    if (hooks.bossDanger) num = Math.max(1, num * 0.05);
    for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {
      // 屏外缓冲 ±600（玩家上跳 ×30，Rain.cs:44-48）
      let margin = 600;
      if (playerVy < 0) margin += Math.floor(Math.abs(playerVy) * 30);
      let x = camX - margin + Math.random() * (viewW + margin * 2);
      const y = camY - (20 + Math.random() * 80);
      x -= w.windSpeedCurrent * 15 * 40;          // 风偏移（Rain.cs:51）
      x += playerVx * 40;                          // 玩家运动补偿
      x = Math.max(0, Math.min((world.w - 1) * TILE, x));
      const tx = Math.max(0, Math.min(world.w - 1, Math.floor(x / TILE)));
      const ty = Math.max(0, Math.min(world.h - 1, Math.floor(y / TILE)));
      // 起点须非实心且无墙（Rain.cs:72-74）
      const st = world.store;
      const solid = st.isSolid(tx, ty);
      const wall = st.wall[st.idx(tx, ty)];
      if (solid || wall > 0) continue;
      this.spawnRain(x, y);
    }
  }

  /** Rain.NewRain（Rain.cs:159-209）：atmo^9 削减 + 雪区平方削减 + 池按 cloudAlpha 限量 */
  private spawnRain(x: number, y: number) {
    const w = this.weather!;
    const hooks = this.hooks!;
    let cap = Math.floor(this.poolCap * w.cloudAlpha);
    const snow = hooks.snowRatio * hooks.snowRatio;
    cap = Math.floor(cap * (1 - snow));
    cap = Math.floor(cap * Math.pow(hooks.atmo, 9));
    if (cap <= 0) return;
    let slot = -1;
    for (let i = 0; i < cap; i++) {
      if (!this.pool[i].active) { slot = i; break; }
    }
    if (slot < 0) return;
    const d = this.pool[slot];
    d.active = true;
    d.x = x; d.y = y;
    d.scale = 1 + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) - 20) * 0.01;
    d.vx = w.windSpeedCurrent * 18 * d.scale;
    d.vy = 14 * d.scale;
    d.rotation = Math.atan2(d.vx, -d.vy);
    d.type = Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);       // waterStyle=0 → type 0-2（Rain.png 三型）

```

Confirmed: animation rates are 4-11 ticks. So in a base with many crafting stations/campfires, `advanceAnim` marks ALL chunks containing those sheets dirty every 4-11 ticks → those chunks get FULLY re-baked (renderChunk = 16×16 tile pass × 2 layers + paint pass + halfbrick pass + tree pass ≈ 1-3ms each). If you have e.g. 20 on-screen chunks with animated tiles all ticking at the same rate (they share global tileAnim.tick, so all campfire chunks tick TOGETHER every 4 ticks), then every 4 ticks you enqueue 20 chunk rebuilds, and flushDirty does only 4/frame → 5 frames to drain, but by then the next tick comes → permanent backlog of ~16 dirty chunks → constant 4 chunk bakes/frame ≈ 4-12ms/frame EVERY FRAME in an animated-tile-dense area. That's a constant frame tax, scales with density, and it's exactly the "帧率随时间劣化"-adjacent chronic issue (worse as you build more animated furniture). CONFIRMED: 中-高.

Mitigation: bake animated tiles into a separate overlay layer (like WindSway) or bake per-frame strips instead of whole chunk.

Also note `markDirty`'s `dirtyQueue.includes` O(n) — with the anim backlog, each additional player edit costs a scan of a ~50-400 entry queue. Minor.

122	      for (let cx = Math.floor(x0 / CHUNK); cx <= Math.floor(x1 / CHUNK); cx++) {

131	    this.markDirty(cx, cy);
132	    // 边缘融合：邻接 chunk 也要标脏
133	    if (x % CHUNK === 0) this.markDirty(cx - 1, cy);
134	    if (x % CHUNK === CHUNK - 1) this.markDirty(cx + 1, cy);
135	    if (y % CHUNK === 0) this.markDirty(cx, cy - 1);
136	    if (y % CHUNK === CHUNK - 1) this.markDirty(cx, cy + 1);
137	  }

141	  invalidateAll(): void {
142	    for (const k of this.chunks.keys()) {
143	      if (this.chunks.get(k) === undefined) continue; // 已是待重建哨兵
144	      this.chunks.set(k, undefined as unknown as ChunkPair);
145	      if (!this.dirtyQueue.includes(k)) this.dirtyQueue.push(k);
146	    }
147	  }

149	  /** 每帧重绘脏 chunk:数量上限 maxN 之外再加时间预算 budgetMs——
150	   *  跑图/全量标脏时烘焙突发不再挤占帧预算(实测 87ms 尖峰来源) */
151	  flushDirty(maxN = 4, budgetMs = 6) {
152	    let n = 0;
153	    const t0 = performance.now();
154	    while (this.dirtyQueue.length && n < maxN) {
155	      const k = this.dirtyQueue.shift()!;
156	      const cx = k & 0xffff, cy = (k >> 16) & 0xffff;
157	      if (this.chunks.get(k) !== undefined) continue; // 已重建
158	      this.get(cx, cy);
159	      n++;
160	      if (performance.now() - t0 > budgetMs) break; // 单 chunk 烘焙超预算也至少完成 1 个
161	    }
162	    this.lastFlushMs = performance.now() - t0;
163	    this.lastFlushCount = n;
164	  }

168	   *  帧内 chunk 复用零开销 */

172	      for (const [sheet, set] of this.animChunksBySheet) {
173	        const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet];
174	        if (!rate || tileAnim.tick % rate !== 0) continue;
175	        for (const k of set) this.markDirty(k & 0xffff, (k >> 16) & 0xffff);
176	      }
177	    }
178	    if (this.animChunksByWall.size) {

180	        const rate = wallAnimRate(wallId);

182	        for (const k of set) this.markDirty(k & 0xffff, (k >> 16) & 0xffff);

185	  }

190	    let c = this.chunks.get(k);
191	    if (c) {
192	      // LRU:命中即刷新 recency(delete+set 移到 Map 尾部)。渲染循环每帧都 get
193	      // 可视 chunk,故屏上 chunk 永远最新、不会被误淘汰
194	      this.chunks.delete(k);
195	      this.chunks.set(k, c);
196	      return c;
197	    }
198	    c = this.renderChunk(cx, cy);
199	    this.chunks.set(k, c);
200	    while (this.chunks.size > ChunkCache.MAX_CHUNKS) {
201	      const oldest = this.chunks.keys().next().value as number | undefined;
202	      if (oldest === undefined) break;
203	      this.chunks.delete(oldest);
204	    }
205	    return c;
206	  }

210	
211	  private neighborMask(x: number, y: number, type: number): number {
212	    const st = this.world.store;
213	    let mask = 0;
214	    const same = (nx: number, ny: number) => st.inBounds(nx, ny) && st.flags[st.idx(nx, ny)] && st.type[st.idx(nx, ny)] === type ? 1 : 0;
215	    mask |= same(x, y - 1);        // N
216	    mask |= same(x + 1, y) << 1;   // E
217	    mask |= same(x, y + 1) << 2;   // S
218	    mask |= same(x - 1, y) << 3;   // W
219	    mask |= same(x + 1, y - 1) << 4; // NE

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T06:01:53.239Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Key new facts:

1. **createRoom (mainFlow.ts:446)**: The production UI path is `MultiplayerSelect.createRoom` (MultiplayerSelect.ts:249-268), which uses `sv.json` — the save JSON already loaded via `listSaves` (loaded from IDB with await, not sync). So NO sync saveGame on the main thread in the production path. `mainFlow.ts:435-468` createRoom with `saveGame(...)` at line 446 is only exposed via the `__swFlow` probe bridge (mainFlow.ts:742). So this is a 误报排除 for production, with a note that the debug bridge does a sync saveGame freeze. Good — correct my earlier finding.

2. **markDirty LRU corruption bug!** Look at markDirty (112-117):
```ts
markDirty(cx, cy) {
  const k = ChunkCache.key(cx, cy);
  if (!this.chunks.has(k)) return;
  this.chunks.set(k, undefined as unknown as ChunkPair); // 标记删除
  ...
}
```
Setting `chunks.set(k, undefined)` on an existing key RE-INSERTS it at the Map tail (moves it to MRU position) AND sets value to undefined. Then `get()`:
```ts
let c = this.chunks.get(k);
if (c) { ...LRU refresh...; return c; }
c = this.renderChunk(cx, cy);
```
`undefined` is falsy → falls to renderChunk → rebuild. OK that works. BUT: the LRU recency is corrupted — a dirty-marked chunk gets moved to the tail (most-recent), so a chunk that's dirty-but-not-yet-rebaked can never be the LRU victim. That's mostly harmless.

But here's a REAL memory anomaly: the eviction loop at 200-204 evicts by deleting from Map — without releasing the canvases. BUT when the evicted chunk is dirty-sentinelled (value undefined), nothing to release, fine. When it's a real ChunkPair, `chunks.delete(oldest)` drops the reference without `pair.tile.width=0`. Confirmed my top finding. Note also: dirty-sentinel entries (`undefined`) count toward `chunks.size` and sit at the Map tail (MRU), so they're protected from eviction — the effective cap of real canvases is reduced and eviction order is skewed. That's a secondary effect worth mentioning.

Wait, actually there's a subtlety: does eviction actually run while dirtyQueue has entries? `invalidateAll` sets ALL entries to undefined → chunks.size unchanged (keys still there) → all values undefined → eviction deletes keys... but the while loop `while (this.chunks.size > MAX)` only triggers on `get()` adding a new chunk. So during invalidateAll storms, no eviction happens at all until new chunks are rendered. And flushDirty's `get()` re-renders → re-adds → may trigger eviction of the OLDEST (possibly a sentinel-undefined entry that's actually the least recently *used*). Evicting a `undefined` sentinel that's still in dirtyQueue leaves a stale dirtyQueue entry whose `get(k) !== undefined` check passes (get returns undefined for missing key)... wait: `if (this.chunks.get(k) !== undefined) continue;` — for an evicted key, `chunks.get(k)` returns undefined → so it does NOT continue → it calls `this.get(cx,cy)` → re-renders. So an evicted chunk gets re-rendered immediately → chunk thrash: evict + re-render loop! If the player is exploring (new chunks on screen) while invalidateAll storms (lazy atlas loads during first exploration), you can evict a chunk that is in dirtyQueue/on-screen and then immediately re-bake it → wasted bakes + canvas allocations without release. This is a genuine death-spiral-ish interaction. Worth listing under the invalidateAll finding as aggravation.

3. **advanceAnim + flushDirty**: `advanceAnim` is called every frame (Game.ts:3568) with `tileAnim.tick++` — tick increments per FRAME (not per 60TPS tick — it's in the render path presumably; line 3567-3568 is in the render/update? Let me not over-claim: it's called once per frame from Game.ts:3567-3568 alongside flushDirty). Rates 4-11 → a sheet's chunks re-marked dirty every 4-11 frames. markDirty pushes into dirtyQueue with `includes` dedup, so no duplicate queue growth. Consumption: 4 chunks/frame max, 6ms budget. If the number of chunks containing animated tiles (campfires, furnaces, crafting stations, animated walls) exceeds ~4 per rate period, there's a permanent backlog → constant re-bake load every frame = chronic frame tax in animated-tile-dense bases. Also every re-bake = full renderChunk (16×16 = 256 tiles × wall+tile passes). And each re-bake while at MAX_CHUNKS... note renderChunk creates NEW canvases each time (renderChunk creates fresh canvas pair presumably) — so re-baked chunks allocate new canvases and the old pair is dropped unreleased! Let me verify renderChunk allocates new canvases. That would be a MASSIVE leak: every animation frame tick allocates 2×256×256 canvases without releasing the old pair.

This is important — let me read renderChunk and get() carefully. In get(): `c = this.renderChunk(cx, cy); this.chunks.set(k, c);` — the old value (undefined sentinel) is replaced. But where is the old pair released? For a dirty-marked chunk, markDirty sets value to undefined but NEVER releases the old pair's canvases! So every re-bake of a dirty chunk leaks the old pair's canvases until GC. And re-bakes happen: on every tile edit (fine, low rate) AND on every animation tick for animated chunks (10-15/sec). THAT is the real memory churn → GPU backing pressure → contextlost storms. The 2026-08-10 incident comment says detached canvas reclamation lags behind GC — so per-animation-tick canvas churn is a chronic GPU pressure source, in exactly the way dispose() documents.

Let me verify renderChunk allocates new canvases.

227	    // 双层画布：墙层 / tile 层分离 —— 水渲染在两层之间（墙之上、图块之下）

238	    // ---- 第一遍：背景墙全部先画（避免后格的墙盖住跨格物体如宝箱/树冠）----

242	      const EXT = 1;
243	      for (let ly = -EXT; ly < CHUNK + EXT; ly++) {
244	        for (let lx = -EXT; lx < CHUNK + EXT; lx++) {
245	          const x = x0 + lx, y = y0 + ly;
246	          if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
247	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
248	          const wallId = st.wall[i];
249	          if (wallId === 0) continue;
250	          const px = lx * TILE, py = ly * TILE;
251	          if (this.wallTiler.hasTexture(wallId)) {
252	            this.wallTiler.draw(ctx, st, x, y, wallId, this.truncatesWalls, px, py);
253	            // 动画墙（DoUpdate_AnimateWalls 换带 + 星彩玻璃逐格错相）：登记进换带
254	            // 重烘焙行列——墙无 sheet 概念，按 wallId 另建 map（tiles 侧同款机制）
255	            if (wallAnimRate(wallId) !== 0) {
256	              let wset = this.animChunksByWall.get(wallId);
257	              if (!wset) { wset = new Set(); this.animChunksByWall.set(wallId, wset); }
258	              wset.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));
259	            }
260	          } else {
261	            const wd = WALL_DEFS[wallId];
262	            if (wd) {
263	              ctx.fillStyle = wd.mapColor;
264	              ctx.fillRect(px, py, TILE, TILE);
265	              ctx.fillStyle = shade(wd.mapColor, 0.8);
266	              ctx.fillRect(px, py + TILE - 1, TILE, 1);
267	              ctx.fillRect(px + TILE - 1, py, 1, TILE);
268	            }
269	          }
270	        }
271	      }
272	    }
273	
274	    // ---- 第二遍：前景 tile / 物体（绘制到 tile 层画布；水渲染在墙层与 tile 层之间）----
275	    ctx = tile.getContext('2d')!;
276	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
277	    for (let ly = 0; ly < CHUNK; ly++) {
278	      for (let lx = 0; lx < CHUNK; lx++) {
279	        const x = x0 + lx, y = y0 + ly;
280	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
281	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
282	        const px = lx * TILE, py = ly * TILE;
283	        const type = st.type[i];
284	        // 原版语义:非活性格不渲染(TileRunner 会给空气格写幽灵 type)
285	        if (type === 0 || !st.flags[i]) continue;
286	        // 已致动(inActive):幽灵态淡显(原版 DrawInactiveSorter,Main.cs:2828 附近)
287	        // 每格开头统一设定 alpha(各 continue 路径无需逐个恢复,下一格自愈)
288	        const actuated = (st.wire[i] & 32) !== 0;
289	        ctx.globalAlpha = actuated ? 0.3 : 1;
290	        const def = TILE_DEFS[type];
291	        if (!def) { ctx.fillStyle = '#808080'; ctx.fillRect(px, py, TILE, TILE); continue; }
292	        // 风摆动图块（草/藤/吊挂植物/树冠标记帧）：摘出静态烘焙，

294	        if (def.vanilla && swayBakeSkip(type, st.frameX[i])) continue;
295	        // 原版素材图块（TileDef.vanilla）：TEdit framing 查找表（auto）或显式 18px 帧（style）
296	        if (def.vanilla && this.autotiler) {
297	          // 动画陈设（原版 AnimateTiles/GetTileDrawData addFrY）：frameY += 帧索引*pitch

299	          let fy = st.frameY[i];
300	          if (def.vanilla.sheet === 215) {
301	            fy += campfireYOffset(st.frameY[i]);
302	            let set215 = this.animChunksBySheet.get(215);
303	            if (!set215) { set215 = new Set(); this.animChunksBySheet.set(215, set215); }
304	            set215.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));
305	          } else if (def.vanilla.sheet === 314) {
306	            // 矿车轨道加速带动画（Main.cs:18734-18741 每 10 tick 推进、5 帧回卷）：
307	            // 只注册重烘焙——frameY 是后轨连接 ID，勿走 addFrY 帧偏移；
308	            // 帧行偏移由 drawMinecartTrackCell → sourceRectOf(frameID, anim) 处理
309	            let set314 = this.animChunksBySheet.get(314);

316	            if (!set) { set = new Set(); this.animChunksBySheet.set(def.vanilla.sheet, set); }
317	            set.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));

321	            def.vanilla.fw ?? 1, def.vanilla.fh ?? 1,

323	            (t) => t === type, // 同 id 融合判定（后续可扩 mergeWith）
324	            px, py, st.frameX[i], fy,
325	            { treeX: this.world.treeX, treeStyle: this.world.treeStyle, treeTops: this.world.treeTops,
326	              worldSurface: this.world.groundLevel, worldW: this.world.w },
327	          );

```
This is inside the game tick (update), not render — `this.chunks.flushDirty()` + `advanceAnim()` are called per game tick (60 TPS fixed step). So:

- advanceAnim tick++ at 60/sec (when running at full speed).
- A campfire (rate 4) → its chunks re-marked dirty 15×/sec.
- flushDirty does max 4 chunks per TICK with a 6ms budget → up to 240 chunks/sec consumption capacity (well above production). So the earlier "4/frame = backlog" concern is mitigated: 4 per 60Hz tick = plenty. Each bake ~0.5-2ms. 6ms budget limits to ~1-3 per tick in practice... hmm, budget 6ms per tick is a big chunk of the 16.6ms frame budget when running 60fps with max 5 catch-up ticks. Actually if the game runs 60 TPS and flushDirty burns up to 6ms EVERY tick, that's 36% of frame budget permanently consumed when there's a sustained backlog. And with campfires at rate 4 → every 4 ticks, all campfire chunks re-marked → if you have N campfire chunks, you need N bakes per 4 ticks = N/4 bakes per tick sustained. Budget allows maybe 2-4 bakes/tick. So sustained backlog if N > ~8-16 chunks with animated tiles. Realistically a base has campfires + furnaces + crafting stations spread across maybe 10-30 chunks → sustained 6ms/tick bake load → chronic frame degradation proportional to animated furniture count. AND the canvas churn: each re-bake allocates 2 fresh canvases (renderChunk:228-231 creates new canvas elements) and drops the old pair without width=0 → GC pressure + GPU backing reclamation lag — the exact mechanism documented in dispose() comment. At 15 rebakes/sec per campfire chunk, that's 30 canvas allocations/sec per chunk, 1800/min. This is a serious chronic leak/GPU-pressure anomaly. CONFIRMED 高.

Wait — but is the old pair actually dropped without release? In markDirty, value is set to undefined (old pair reference dropped). In flushDirty → get() → renderChunk creates new pair. Old pair was already dropped at markDirty time. So yes, the old canvases become detached garbage each rebake, relying on GC. The dispose() comment explicitly says GC reclamation of detached canvases lags and caused the contextlost storm. So this is the same failure mode at runtime, driven by animation rebakes. This is the strongest finding. Severity 高.

Also `this.chunks.set(k, undefined)` on an EXISTING key re-inserts at Map tail (MRU) — dirty chunks are shielded from LRU eviction; and more importantly eviction loop deletes keys without releasing canvases (ChunkCache.ts:200-204). Both confirmed.

One more check on the invalidateAll frequency claim (Game.ts:1700-1703). I already have that from the summary. Good.

I now have everything to write the final report. Also note flushDirty is called per game tick with maxN=4, budget 6ms — during catch-up (5 ticks/frame after a hitch) that's up to 30ms of baking in one frame → post-hitch stutter amplification. Worth mentioning as a compounding factor.

Let me also double check the `Sfx.setRain` call site claim quickly? I have it from summary (Game.ts:7899 every tick). Fine.

Now write the final three-tier report in Chinese, with 文件:行号 evidence, severity, cheapest fix direction. No code. Keep it organized and reasonably concise but complete.

# SandboxWorld 性能异常态扫描报告

## 【确认异常】(证据 + 触发条件 + 影响)

### 1. ChunkCache 重烘焙路径每 4-11 tick 丢弃旧 canvas 对,不释放 GPU 背板 —— 慢性显存劣化,与 2026-08-10 contextlost 风暴同机制 — **高**

- 证据:
  - `renderChunk` 每次烘焙都新建两张 256² canvas:`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/ChunkCache.ts:226-231`
  - `markDirty` 把旧 pair 直接置 `undefined` 丢弃引用,不调用 `width=0`:`ChunkCache.ts:112-117`(`this.chunks.set(k, undefined ...)`)
  - `advanceAnim` 按 `TILE_ANIM_RATE`(篝火 215:4、熔炉 17/77:6、工作台族 5、加速带 314:10、恶魔祭坛 8 等)周期性把含动画 tile 的 chunk 重新标脏:`ChunkCache.ts:169-185` + `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/TileAnim.ts`(rate 表)
  - 每游戏 tick 调用一次:`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/Game.ts:3567-3568`
  - 而 `dispose()` 的注释明确写着:detached canvas 回收依赖 GC 且明显滞后,已实证引发 contextlost/contextrestored 风暴卡死:`ChunkCache.ts:82-84`
- 触发条件:屏幕内/缓存内存在动画陈设(篝火、熔炉、凝固机、制冰机、合成台、加速轨道带、动画墙)。篝火 rate=4 → 该 chunk 每秒 15 次重烘焙 = 每秒 30 张 256² canvas 变成 detached 垃圾。
- 影响:常态 GC 压力 + GPU 背板回收滞后。据仓库自己的事故注释,这正是"连续多次读档累积数百 MB 显存 → contextlost 风暴"的机制,只是这里把触发源从"读档"换成了"放置动画家具"。放置越多篝火/熔炉,劣化越快 —— 典型慢性劣化,符合"帧率随时间变差"的表症。
- 最便宜修法方向:重烘焙时先 `old.tile.width = old.wall.width = 0` 再替换(一处改动);或动画 tile 摘出 chunk 烘焙、走类似 WindSway 的 overlay 逐帧绘制(根治)。

### 2. ChunkCache LRU 淘汰已加上限,但淘汰分支不释放 canvas — **高**

- 证据:`ChunkCache.ts:200-204` —— `this.chunks.delete(oldest)` 后没有任何 `pair.wall.width=0`/`pair.tile.width=0`,与 `dispose()`(`:85-94`)的释放逻辑不一致。
- 触发条件:缓存超过 384 chunk(跑图 / 相机大幅平移 / 尺度调大),且新 chunk 进入触发淘汰。
- 影响:淘汰掉的 chunk 全部变成 detached canvas,回到第 1 条同样的 GC 滞后路径。原"无淘汰"问题已修(`MAX_CHUNKS=384`,`:77`),但修漏了一半 —— 上限挡住了数量,没挡住释放。
- 附带次级问题:`markDirty`/`invalidateAll` 用 `chunks.set(k, undefined)` 标脏会把该 key 移到 Map 尾部(MRU),被标脏的 chunk 永远不会被 LRU 淘汰,且 `undefined` 哨兵占 `chunks.size`,384 的实际上限被侵蚀;`dirtyQueue.includes` 是 O(n) 线性去重(`:116`、`:145`),`invalidateAll` 全量标脏 + 后续逐个 markDirty 时是 O(n²)。
- 最便宜修法方向:淘汰循环里复用 `dispose()` 的释放逻辑;去重改一个 `Set` 伴生表。

### 3. `AudioSystem.buffers` 永不释放已解码 BGM PCM — **高**(长会话内存头号来源)

- 证据:`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/Audio.ts:19` —— `private buffers = new Map<number, AudioBuffer>()`,成功解码即常驻;`pool` 里的播放实例在淡出后会被 stop+delete(这部分是好的),但 buffers 不随之回收。
- 量化:public/audios/music 共 104 个 mp3 / 160MB,平均约 90-100 秒/首;44.1kHz stereo float32 解码后约 30-45MB/首。会话内循环切过 8-10 首歌即常驻 300MB+。
- 触发条件:长时间游玩,白天/夜晚/Boss/地下等音乐场景来回切换。切歌本身是异步 fetch+decode,不卡帧 —— 纯内存劣化,最终在低显存/低内存设备上挤爆引发卡顿。
- 最便宜修法方向:解码缓存加 LRU 上限 2-3 首(淡出归零时淘汰);或解码降采样/单声道。

### 4. atlas 懒加载回调整体 `invalidateAll`,探索新生态时形成重烘焙风暴 — **中偏高**

- 证据:`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/Game.ts:1700-1703` —— `onVImageLoaded` 每加载一张 `vanilla/Tiles_*`/`vanilla/Wall_*` 就调用 `this.chunks.invalidateAll()`(全缓存 ≤384 chunk 重新入队,且逐 key 走 `dirtyQueue.includes` O(n) 去重)。资源侧有 765 张 `Tiles_*` + 368 张 `Wall_*` PNG。
- 触发条件:首次进新生态/新 biome,lazy atlas 陆续到位的几分钟窗口。每次 invalidateAll 把已烘焙 chunk 里的内容作废重烘,配合 `flushDirty(maxN=4, budgetMs=6)`(`ChunkCache.ts:151`)以每 tick 6ms 的速度重灌 384 个 chunk。
- 影响:探索期的持续性帧税 + 与第 1/2 条叠加放大 canvas 丢弃量;之后 `advanceAnim` 又叠加周期性重烘焙。
- 最便宜修法方向:新表到位时只重烘"真正用到该 sheet 的 chunk"(已存在 `animChunksBySheet` 按表分组的先例,可维护 sheet→chunks 反查),而不是全量;或给 invalidateAll 加去抖(收集一批文件后一次性)。

### 5. `HitTile.update()` 每 2 tick 全量复制 Map entries — **中**

- 证据:`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/HitTile.ts:68` —— `for (const [key, e] of [...this.data])`;上限 500 条 + TTL 清理(上限本身没问题)。
- 触发条件:持续挖矿(挖掘热区 hitTiles 接近满),30 次/秒 × 最多 500 entry 数组拷贝。
- 影响:稳态无感、挖矿时持续产生 500 长度数组 + 500 个元组,加大 minor GC 频率。
- 最便宜修法方向:去掉拷贝直接迭代(JS Map 迭代中 delete 当前项是安全的),或 TTL 改为按 tick 对齐的桶。

### 6. 粒子更新循环内每粒子每 tick 分配闭包 — **中**

- 证据:`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/Game.ts:3760-3783` —— `const st = this.world.store` 与 `const solidAt = (x,y) => {...}` 都在 `for (const p of this.particles)` 循环体内。
- 触发条件:粒子接近 600 上限时(爆炸、陨石、液体交互),60TPS × 600 ≈ 36,000 闭包/秒。
- 影响:纯分配 churn,G9 已修掉 dmgNumbers/particles 的数组复制,这是残留的一处。
- 最便宜修法方向:把 `st`/`solidAt` 提到循环外(签名相同,零行为变化)。

### 7. `LightingEngine.lightAt` 每次调用返回新数组 — **中低**

- 证据:`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/lighting/LightingEngine.ts:160`(返回新 `[r,g,b]`);调用方包括每滴雨每帧一次 `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/WeatherRenderer.ts:196`、敌人血条 `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/Renderer.ts:1621`;`perFrame` 数组本体每帧 `length=0` 复用(`:137`,不漏)。
- 触发条件:雨天(雨滴数量随屏宽)+ 屏上多个敌人血条。
- 影响:雨天持续数千小数组/秒,叠加 rain drop 自身的 save/restore+drawImage,是雨天掉帧的组成部分。
- 最便宜修法方向:加一个 `lightAtInto(tx,ty,out)` 写入调用方复用的数组。

### 8. 移动端摇杆 touchmove 每次事件读布局 + 转场过渡重启 — **中**(仅移动端)

- 证据:`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/ui/MobileControls.ts:189-198` —— `move()` 内 `joy.getBoundingClientRect()` + `knob.style.transform` 模板串写;且 `.sw-mjoy` CSS 有 `transition:transform 90ms ease`(`:39`),每次 move 都重启过渡。
- 触发条件:触屏设备拖动摇杆(60-120Hz 触控采样)。
- 影响:输入频率下强制 layout 读取 + 每事件样式写 + 过渡重启动画合成,移动端持续小抖动来源;桌面零影响(整个层不渲染)。
- 最便宜修法方向:touchstart 时缓存 rect(布局稳定);拖动期间给 knob 关掉 transition。

### 9. `geyserTiles` Set 增量维护缺失 — **低**(性能轻微,含正确性问题)

- 证据:`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/Game.ts:1348`(声明)、`:7916-7936`(`scanTriggerTiles` 全量扫描,但只清 `triggerTiles428/423` 不清 geyserTiles);增量 `onTileChanged` 监听只处理 sheet 428/423,不处理 443(间歇泉)。
- 触发条件:挖掉/放置间歇泉后,Set 里留下永久失效索引,新泉不入册。
- 影响:条目随开图增长(全图尺寸量级,几千以内),性能影响小;主要是新泉不喷、旧索引残留。
- 最便宜修法方向:scan 时一并 clear geyserTiles,onTileChanged 补 443 分支。

### 10. 追帧放大重烘焙:catch-up 一次最多 5 tick × 6ms 预算 — **中低**(交互后二次卡顿的放大器)

- 证据:`flushDirty(maxN=4, budgetMs=6)` 在 update 内调用(`Game.ts:3567`、`ChunkCache.ts:151-164`);固定步长循环单帧最多补 5 tick。
- 触发条件:任何一次 GC/掉帧后,下一帧最多连跑 5 次 flushDirty → 单帧最多 30ms 烘焙。
- 影响:一次小卡顿被放大成更长卡顿(dirtyQueue 有积压时)。
- 最便宜修法方向:flushDirty 换到渲染路径每帧只调一次,或补帧时跳过烘焙。

---

## 【疑似】(需复现确认)

### 1. `tintCache` 满 1024 清空的"惊群"停顿 — **中**
`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/Renderer.ts:4390` 附近(1024 上限 + 整表 `clear()`)。一旦触顶,下一帧要为屏上所有染色精灵重建 tint canvas,瞬时尖峰。`BiomeBackground` 的 64 上限同款(`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/BiomeBackground.ts:453`),且其 key 含 `toFixed(2)` 的连续色值 —— 若地表背景 tint 随昼夜连续插值,缓存基本永不命中,每帧新建大尺寸(背景图自然宽最高 2048)tint canvas。修法方向:LRU 淘汰替代整表清空;背景 tint 色值量化到步长 1/16。

### 2. 动画陈设密集区的持续重烘焙税 — **中高,机制已确认、量级需实测**
`TILE_ANIM_RATE` 4-6 居多(TileAnim.ts)→ 篝火族 chunk 每 4 tick 重烘焙。`flushDirty` 每 tick 最多 4 个 + 6ms 预算;若屏内含动画 tile 的 chunk 超过约 8-16 个(大型基地:多篝火+多熔炉+成排工作台+动画墙),重烘焙生产速率 > 消费速率 → `dirtyQueue` 常态积压 → 每 tick 都烧满 6ms 预算 = 持续约 36% 帧预算税,且叠加第 1 条的 canvas 丢弃。触发条件明确(动画家具密度),建议实测 F5 调试面板的 `lastFlushMs/lastFlushCount`(已有埋点,`ChunkCache.ts:78-80`)。

### 3. Minimap `flushDirty` 逐像素 fillRect 消化不动 — **中低**
`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/Renderer.ts:708-726`:每帧上限 24 chunk,每 chunk 256 次 `fillStyle` 字符串 + `fillRect(1px)` ≈ 6144 次/帧,而液体流动/挖掘时标脏速率更高 → `dirtyChunks` 积压(上限为全图 chunk 数,大世界约 1.9 万,不漏但延迟明显)。表现:小地图/全图在大量动水时滞后数秒。修法方向:改 ImageData/Uint32 直写(已有 `redrawAll` 的 LUT 先例)。

### 4. SpriteAtlas `vimages` 单调内存下限 — **低中**
`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/assets/SpriteAtlas.ts`(vimages Map 无淘汰,public/sprites/vanilla 4245 文件/50MB 磁盘)。因是懒加载,只有真正见过的表会驻留,但长会话跨多生态后趋近全量。非泄漏、是"地板抬升"。修法方向:与 ChunkCache LRU 共用同一套淘汰策略,或接受并在低端机做总预算。

### 5. `Sfx.setRain` 每 tick 提交音频参数事件 — **低**
`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/Sfx.ts:148-174`,Game 每 tick 调用(`Game.ts:7899`),`setTargetAtTime` 每次都提交(60 次/秒),即使音量没变。修法方向:epsilon 早退。

### 6. 沙落队列 `shift()` × 128/tick — **低**
`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/Game.ts:9333`(Array push)、`:12146-12147`(`shift()` 循环,上限 128/tick)。大范围落沙连锁时 O(n) 头部弹出 + 每格元组分配。修法方向:环形缓冲或读指针前移。

---

## 优先级排序(按"修一处收益/成本比")

1. **确认异常 #1 + #2**(同一处根因:detached canvas 不释放)—— 修法是几行 `width=0`,直接掐断慢性显存劣化主通道。
2. **确认异常 #3**(Audio buffers LRU)—— 一处小改,回收数百 MB。
3. **确认异常 #4**(invalidateAll 精确化)—— 消除探索期重烘焙风暴。
4. **疑似 #2 + 确认 #10** —— 用现成 `lastFlushMs/lastFlushCount` 埋点实测后再决定是否上动画 overlay 层。
5. 其余(闭包提升、HitTile 拷贝、lightAtInto、摇杆 rect 缓存)都是单点微改,可随手带走。
```
